    50	/// Phase 2A.6 audit H1/L4 (2026-05-12) helper. Confirms `sheet` is in range
    51	/// before any work that would otherwise panic inside `Workbook::put_at`.
    52	/// `pub(crate)` so `WorkbookTransaction` can call the same validator at
    53	/// `put_value` / `put_formula` buffering time.
    54	pub(crate) fn validate_sheet(workbook: &Workbook, sheet: SheetId) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    55	    let count = workbook.sheet_count();
    56	    if (sheet as usize) >= count {
    57	        return Err(RuntimeError::InvalidSheet {
    58	            sheet,
    59	            sheet_count: count,
    60	        });
    61	    }
    62	    Ok(())
    63	}
    64	
    65	/// Phase 2A.7 audit H1 (2026-05-12) helper. Combines sheet validation with
    66	/// row/col bounds checks so the runtime entry points refuse out-of-grid
    67	/// coordinates before any work that would otherwise panic inside
    68	/// `Sheet::put` (which asserts `row <= MAX_ROW && col <= MAX_COLUMN`). The
    69	/// Phase 2A.6 audit closed sheet-id panics but missed row/col — three
    70	/// independent megaudit agents flagged the gap. `pub(crate)` so
    71	/// `WorkbookTransaction` can call the same validator at buffer time.
    72	/// **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** rewrite cross-sheet refs in a formula text
    73	/// when a sheet is renamed. Round-trips `text → lex → parse → rewrite
    74	/// → print` so substitution is tokenization-aware (string literals,
    75	/// quoted-sheet escapes, function calls, etc. all get the right
    76	/// treatment).
    77	///
    78	/// Returns `Some(new_text)` if the rewrite changed the formula,
    79	/// `None` otherwise (so the caller can skip the storage write +
    80	/// op-log entry when there's nothing to record).
    81	///
    82	/// Defensive: if `lex` or `parse` fails (corrupted historical text),
    83	/// returns `None` rather than propagating an error — the rename
    84	/// shouldn't break otherwise-recoverable workbooks.
    85	fn rewrite_formula_text_for_sheet_rename(
    86	    text: &str,
    87	    old_canonical: &str,
    88	    new_name: &std::sync::Arc<str>,
    89	) -> Option<String> {
    90	    let stripped = text.strip_prefix('=').unwrap_or(text);
    91	    let tokens = ql_formula_syntax::lex(stripped).ok()?;
    92	    let expr = ql_formula_syntax::parse(tokens).ok()?;
    93	    let rewritten = ql_formula_syntax::rewrite_sheet_name_in_expr(&expr, old_canonical, new_name);
    94	    if rewritten == expr {
    95	        // Nothing changed; preserve the original text (avoids spurious
    96	        // op-log entries and skips the round-trip-printing's canonical-
    97	        // form rewrites for formulas that have no sheet references).
    98	        return None;
    99	    }
   100	    let printed = ql_formula_syntax::print(&rewritten);
   101	    // Preserve a leading `=` if the original had one.
   102	    let with_eq = if text.starts_with('=') {
   103	        format!("={printed}")
   104	    } else {
   105	        printed
   106	    };
   107	    Some(with_eq)
   108	}
   109	
   110	pub(crate) fn validate_cell(
   111	    workbook: &Workbook,
   112	    sheet: SheetId,
   113	    row: RowId,
   114	    col: ColId,
   115	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   116	    validate_sheet(workbook, sheet)?;
   117	    if row > MAX_ROW {
   118	        return Err(RuntimeError::InvalidCell {
   119	            sheet,
   120	            row,
   121	            col,
   122	            why: "row exceeds MAX_ROW (1,048,575)",
   123	        });
   124	    }
   125	    if col > MAX_COLUMN {
   126	        return Err(RuntimeError::InvalidCell {
   127	            sheet,
   128	            row,
   129	            col,
   130	            why: "col exceeds MAX_COLUMN (16,383)",
   131	        });
   132	    }
   133	    Ok(())
   134	}
   135	
   136	/// Errors from the runtime pipeline. Each upstream stage's error wraps cleanly.
   137	/// Phase 2A.6 audit H1/L4 (2026-05-12) added `InvalidSheet` so callers that pass
   138	/// a sheet id outside the workbook's range get a clean error instead of a panic
   139	/// from `Workbook::put_at`. Phase 2A.6 audit H4 added `ConflictingOps` for
   140	/// the `WorkbookTransaction` mixed-kind-on-same-cell case.
   141	#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   142	pub enum RuntimeError {
   143	    // Phase 2A.11 audit M16: switched from `{0:?}` debug formatters to `{0}`
   144	    // Display now that LexError + BindError implement thiserror::Error with
   145	    // user-facing strings. IDE error display reads cleanly: "lex error:
   146	    // unexpected character: '@'" instead of "lex error: UnexpectedChar('@')".
   147	    #[error("lex error: {0}")]
   148	    Lex(LexError),
   149	
   150	    #[error("parse error: {0}")]
   151	    Parse(#[from] ParseError),
   152	
   153	    #[error("bind error: {0}")]
   154	    Bind(BindError),
   155	
   156	    #[error("invalid sheet {sheet}: workbook has {sheet_count} sheets")]
   157	    InvalidSheet { sheet: SheetId, sheet_count: usize },
   158	
   159	    /// Phase 2A.7 audit H1: row/col outside the Excel grid (MAX_ROW = 1,048,575;
   160	    /// MAX_COLUMN = 16,383). Surfaces at buffer time so `Sheet::put` doesn't
   161	    /// panic mid-commit. `why` describes which bound was exceeded.
   162	    #[error("invalid cell (sheet={sheet}, row={row}, col={col}): {why}")]
   163	    InvalidCell {
   164	        sheet: SheetId,
   165	        row: RowId,
   166	        col: ColId,
   167	        why: &'static str,
   168	    },
   169	
   170	    #[error(
   171	        "conflicting transaction ops on cell (sheet={sheet}, row={row}, col={col}): \
   172	         a value and a formula were both buffered for this cell in the same transaction"
   173	    )]
   174	    ConflictingOps {
   175	        sheet: SheetId,
   176	        row: RowId,
   177	        col: ColId,
   178	    },
   179	
   180	    /// Phase 2A.3.b (2026-05-12): the producer-side append into an attached
   181	    /// op log failed (serde_json refused the wire value — e.g. NaN/Inf — or
   182	    /// Loro surfaced an internal error). Producer order is append-then-mutate:
   183	    /// when this fires, the workbook is unchanged. Callers attaching an op
   184	    /// log handle the error; callers that don't attach one never see this
   185	    /// variant.
   186	    #[error("op log error: {0}")]
   187	    OpLog(#[from] ql_oplog::OpLogError),
   188	
   189	    /// Phase 2B.5 (2026-05-12): `WorkbookRuntime::set_name` couldn't register
   190	    /// the name in the workbook's `NameTable`. Currently the only
   191	    /// `NameTableError` variant is `Reserved` (per CORR-06 the `AI` name is
   192	    /// engine-reserved); see `ql_storage::NameTableError` for additional
   193	    /// variants when they land.
   194	    #[error("name table error: {0}")]
   195	    Name(#[from] ql_storage::NameTableError),
   196	
   197	    /// **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** sheet-name validation refused — empty
   198	    /// string, Excel-reserved character, or canonical duplicate.
   199	    /// Surfaces from `WorkbookRuntime::rename_sheet` (and future
   200	    /// `add_sheet` variants that validate).
   201	    #[error("sheet name rejected: {0}")]
   202	    SheetName(#[from] ql_storage::SheetNameError),
   203	
   204	    /// Phase 2B.7 audit H1 (2026-05-12): `WorkbookRuntime::add_sheet` was
   205	    /// called with `chunk_rows == 0`. `Sheet::with_chunk_rows` accepts it
   206	    /// silently, then the first cell write panics deep inside the column
   207	    /// store. Pre-validating at the runtime entry refuses the value cleanly
   208	    /// before any op-log append, so a bad input can't poison a workbook.
   209	    #[error("invalid chunk_rows {0}: must be > 0 (engine default is 16384)")]
   210	    InvalidChunkRows(u32),
   211	
   212	    /// Phase 2B.7 audit H1 (2026-05-12): `WorkbookRuntime::add_sheet` was
   213	    /// called with the workbook already at `SheetId::MAX` (65,535) sheets.
   214	    /// `Workbook::add_sheet_with_chunk_rows` panics in that case; the
   215	    /// runtime now refuses before any op-log append.
   216	    #[error("workbook has {current} sheets; cannot add another (limit {max})")]
   217	    TooManySheets { current: u32, max: u32 },
   218	
   219	    /// **W5-82 (Phase 4.5.D part 6):** `set_cell_format` was called with
   220	    /// `Some(FormatId)` referencing an id that hasn't been registered
   221	    /// in the workbook's `FormatTable`. Producer-side enforcement of the
   222	    /// "RegisterFormat before SetCellFormat" ordering; mirrors replay's
   223	    /// `FormatNotRegistered`. Callers should obtain the id via
   224	    /// `intern_format` (which handles registration automatically).
   225	    #[error("format id {0} is not registered in the workbook FormatTable")]
   226	    UnknownFormatId(u32),
   227	}
   228	
   229	impl From<LexError> for RuntimeError {
   230	    fn from(e: LexError) -> Self {
   231	        Self::Lex(e)
   232	    }
   233	}
   234	
   235	impl From<BindError> for RuntimeError {
   236	    fn from(e: BindError) -> Self {
   237	        Self::Bind(e)
   238	    }
   239	}
   240	
   241	/// Outcome of a single failed cell during `WorkbookRuntime::recompute_all`.
   242	///
   243	/// Phase 2B.2 (2026-05-12): part of the [`RecomputeResult`] aggregation
   244	/// replacing the prior short-circuit `Result<usize, RuntimeError>` return
   245	/// shape. Captures everything a caller needs to display the failure
   246	/// without re-walking the workbook: the cell address, the formula text
   247	/// that was tried, and the structural error from lex/parse/bind.
   248	///
   249	/// Evaluation-time errors that surface as `Value::Error(...)` (e.g. `=10/0`
   250	/// → `#DIV/0!`) do NOT appear here — those are normal cell values per
   251	/// Excel canon and get written to the workbook like any other recompute
   252	/// success. `RecomputeFailure` is reserved for STRUCTURAL failures where
   253	/// the formula cannot be evaluated at all.
   254	///
   255	/// Not `Clone` — `RuntimeError` contains `loro::LoroError` (via the
   256	/// Phase 2A.3.b `OpLog` variant) which is not Clone. Pass by ownership
   257	/// or borrow.
   258	#[derive(Debug)]
   259	pub struct RecomputeFailure {
   260	    pub sheet: SheetId,
   261	    pub row: RowId,
   262	    pub col: ColId,
   263	    pub formula_text: Arc<str>,
   264	    pub error: RuntimeError,
   265	}
   266	
   267	/// Aggregate outcome of `WorkbookRuntime::recompute_all`.
   268	///
   269	/// Phase 2B.2 (2026-05-12): replaces the prior `Result<usize, RuntimeError>`
   270	/// return shape. The previous signature short-circuited on first failure,
   271	/// dropping per-cell context and forcing callers to re-walk the workbook
   272	/// to find what went wrong. The new shape:
   273	///
   274	/// - `attempted` — total formula cells in the workbook snapshot at start.
   275	/// - `succeeded` — formulas that lex/parse/bind/eval'd cleanly and were
   276	///   written back to the workbook.
   277	/// - `failures` — every formula that failed structurally, in iteration
   278	///   order. Each entry carries cell address + formula text + error.
   279	///
   280	/// "Is this workbook now consistent?" is exposed via `is_complete()`
   281	/// (= `failures.is_empty()`); see `failed_count()` for the count.
   282	/// Phase 2B.7 audit (correctness M2) dropped a redundant `partial_state`
   283	/// `pub` field that mirrored `!is_complete()` — having two sources of
   284	/// truth created a soundness footgun (external constructors could lie).
   285	///
   286	/// No-fallback semantics: failures are NOT swallowed. The struct makes
   287	/// every failure visible and aggregable. Callers that want short-circuit
   288	/// semantics can check `is_complete()` and bail.
   289	///
   290	/// Cells past a failure point keep their pre-recompute (potentially stale)
   291	/// values; their formula text is preserved either way (recompute never
   292	/// clears formula on failure). Iteration order is `HashMap`-arbitrary
   293	/// today (GAP-R-01); Engine Phase 3 calcgraph integration makes it
   294	/// topological.
   295	///
   296	/// Not `Clone` for the same reason as `RecomputeFailure`.
   297	///
   298	/// Phase 3.8 (W5-41, 2026-05-12) adds the `skipped_value_equality` field
   299	/// — VEQ-3-03's "profile records skipped downstream vertices." A dirty
   300	/// formula whose direct cell deps all stayed at their prior values, and
   301	/// which has no range deps or volatile flag, is skipped entirely during
   302	/// `recompute_dirty`. The counter is `0` for `recompute_all` (which
   303	/// always processes every formula in HashMap order — no VEQ logic).
   304	///
   305	/// Phase 3.9 (W5-42, 2026-05-12) adds `simd_classified` — SIMD-3-03's
   306	/// "graph profile shows region execution." Counts how many of the dirty
   307	/// formulas had plans that `lower::classify` says are SIMD-eligible
   308	/// (`SimdShape != NotApplicable`). V1 of Phase 3.9 doesn't yet batch
   309	/// these into bulk Arrow kernel calls (that's the Phase 4.7+ array-
   310	/// formula / FormulaRegion binder work); the count is the observability
   311	/// surface that proves the graph scheduler SEES region-eligible cells.
   312	#[derive(Debug)]
   313	pub struct RecomputeResult {
   314	    pub attempted: usize,
   315	    pub succeeded: usize,
   316	    pub failures: Vec<RecomputeFailure>,
   317	    /// Phase 3.8 (VEQ-3-03): count of formulas that were in the dirty
   318	    /// set but skipped because no upstream value actually changed.
   319	    /// Always `0` for the legacy `recompute_all` path.
   320	    pub skipped_value_equality: usize,
   321	    /// Phase 3.9 (SIMD-3-03): count of dirty formulas whose plan
   322	    /// `lower::classify` recognized as SIMD-eligible (a recognized
   323	    /// `SimdShape`). V1 ships observability only — the bulk SIMD
   324	    /// dispatch via `simd::*` lives on the bench / FormulaRegion path
   325	    /// (Phase 4.7+); this counter proves the graph scheduler is aware
   326	    /// of region-eligible formulas. Always `0` for `recompute_all`.
   327	    pub simd_classified: usize,
   328	}
   329	
   330	impl RecomputeResult {
   331	    /// True iff every formula recomputed without a structural failure.
   332	    pub fn is_complete(&self) -> bool {
   333	        self.failures.is_empty()
   334	    }
   335	
   336	    /// Number of structural failures (lex/parse/bind). Excludes evaluation
   337	    /// errors like `#DIV/0!` which are normal cell values.
   338	    pub fn failed_count(&self) -> usize {
   339	        self.failures.len()
   340	    }
   341	}
   342	
   343	/// Live-formula facade. Wraps a `&mut Workbook` + `&FunctionRegistry`.
   344	///
   345	/// Construct one per session of cell edits. Re-creating per call is cheap (the
   346	/// struct holds borrows, no heap state of its own).
   347	///
   348	/// Phase 2A.3.b (2026-05-12): optionally attach an `OpLog` via
   349	/// `WorkbookRuntime::with_oplog`. When attached, `set_value` and `set_formula`
   350	/// emit ops into the log in append-before-mutate order. `transaction()`
   351	/// re-borrows the op-log handle into the returned transaction so a single op
   352	/// log records both individual edits and batched commits cohesively.
   353	pub struct WorkbookRuntime<'a> {
   354	    workbook: &'a mut Workbook,
   355	    registry: &'a FunctionRegistry,
   356	    /// Optional op-log sink. When `Some`, all producer methods append before
   357	    /// mutating the workbook so a failing append leaves the workbook
   358	    /// unchanged. When `None`, the runtime behaves exactly as Phase 1 W5-10.
   359	    oplog: Option<&'a mut OpLog>,
   360	    /// Phase 2B.3 bind-plan cache. Lives for the runtime's lifetime;
   361	    /// successive `set_formula` and `recompute_all` calls hit the cache
   362	    /// for unchanged formula text under the same NameTable generation.
   363	    /// See `crate::plan_cache` module docs for the invalidation contract.
   364	    plan_cache: PlanCache,
   365	    /// Phase 3.1 (2026-05-12) optional calcgraph session. When `Some`,
   366	    /// every mutation method calls the corresponding hook on the
   367	    /// session AFTER the workbook mutation succeeds. Phase 3.1 hooks
   368	    /// are stubs (counter bumps + cell-index updates); Phase 3.3 wires
   369	    /// dirty propagation through the same surface. See
   370	    /// `crate::calcgraph_session` module docs for the ownership model.
   371	    graph: Option<&'a mut crate::CalcgraphSession>,
   372	    /// **W5-82 (Phase 4.5.D part 6):** parsed `FormatString` cache keyed
   373	    /// by `FormatId`. `read_display` populates on-demand from
   374	    /// `Workbook::formats()` lookup; `FormatTable::register_at` rejects
   375	    /// id→string mutations, so cache entries stay stable for the
   376	    /// runtime's lifetime. A new `RegisterFormat` op merely inserts a
   377	    /// new entry; no invalidation needed.
   378	    format_cache: std::collections::HashMap<FormatId, FormatString>,
   379	}
   380	
   381	impl<'a> WorkbookRuntime<'a> {
   382	    pub fn new(workbook: &'a mut Workbook, registry: &'a FunctionRegistry) -> Self {
   383	        Self {
   384	            workbook,
   385	            registry,
   386	            oplog: None,
   387	            plan_cache: PlanCache::new(),
   388	            format_cache: std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   389	            graph: None,
   390	        }
   391	    }
   392	
   393	    /// Phase 2A.3.b: construct a runtime that records every mutation to the
   394	    /// supplied `OpLog`. Producer-replay equivalence: replaying the resulting
   395	    /// log against a fresh workbook (then calling `recompute_all` to
   396	    /// materialize formula values) reproduces the same observable state.
   397	    ///
   398	    /// See module docs for the documented limitations: `set_value(Value::Blank)`
   399	    /// emits no `PutValue` (CellWireValue lacks a Blank variant in 2A.3.b);
   400	    /// NaN/Inf in number values fail serde_json serialization and surface as
   401	    /// `RuntimeError::OpLog`.
   402	    pub fn with_oplog(
   403	        workbook: &'a mut Workbook,
   404	        registry: &'a FunctionRegistry,
   405	        oplog: &'a mut OpLog,
   406	    ) -> Self {
   407	        Self {
   408	            workbook,
   409	            registry,
   410	            oplog: Some(oplog),
   411	            plan_cache: PlanCache::new(),
   412	            format_cache: std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   413	            graph: None,
   414	        }
   415	    }
   416	
   417	    /// Phase 3.1 (2026-05-12): construct a runtime that fires calcgraph
   418	    /// mutation hooks on every producer method. Phase 3.1 hooks are
   419	    /// stubs (counter bumps + cell-index updates) — they accumulate
   420	    /// state that Phase 3.3 will turn into real dirty propagation and

     1	//! The `Op` enum — the engine's mutation vocabulary for the op log.
     2	//!
     3	//! Phase 2A.3.a (2026-05-12): minimum producer set covering everything
     4	//! `WorkbookRuntime` and `WorkbookTransaction` can mutate through their
     5	//! public API. Future variants (sheet renames, sheet deletes, formatting
     6	//! changes) land alongside the API additions that produce them.
     7	//!
     8	//! Each variant mirrors a `Workbook` mutation method one-to-one:
     9	//!
    10	//! | `Op` variant       | Replays to                                       |
    11	//! |--------------------|--------------------------------------------------|
    12	//! | `PutValue`         | `Workbook::put_at`                               |
    13	//! | `PutFormula`       | `Workbook::put_formula`                          |
    14	//! | `ClearFormula`     | `Workbook::clear_formula`                        |
    15	//! | `SetName`          | `Workbook::set_name`                             |
    16	//! | `AddSheet`         | `Workbook::add_sheet_with_chunk_rows`            |
    17	//! | `RegisterFormat`   | `FormatTable::register_at` (W5-80)               |
    18	//! | `SetCellFormat`    | `CellFormatOverlay::set` / `::clear` (W5-80)     |
    19	//! | `BatchCommit`      | (recursive — applies each inner op in order)     |
    20	//!
    21	//! ## Wire format choices
    22	//!
    23	//! - `Arc<str>` is NOT used in wire fields. `serde` without the `rc`
    24	//!   feature can't deserialize `Arc<str>` directly. We store `String` on
    25	//!   the wire and convert at the replay boundary. Cleaner separation: the
    26	//!   wire format owns its strings; the runtime types share via Arc once
    27	//!   inside the workbook.
    28	//! - `ValueWire` and `NamedTargetWire` are reused from
    29	//!   `ql_io::qbook_format` (Phase 2A.8 shipped them). Sharing the
    30	//!   serialization vocabulary across the engine's three persistence
    31	//!   surfaces (qbook envelope, qbook JSONL, op log) prevents wire-format
    32	//!   drift.
    33	
    34	use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
    35	
    36	use ql_io::{CellWireValue, NamedTargetWire};
    37	use ql_types::{ColId, RowId, SheetId};
    38	
    39	/// A mutation operation recordable in the op log.
    40	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    41	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields, tag = "kind")]
    42	pub enum Op {
    43	    /// Single literal cell write. Mirrors `WorkbookRuntime::set_value`.
    44	    PutValue {
    45	        sheet: SheetId,
    46	        row: RowId,
    47	        col: ColId,
    48	        value: CellWireValue,
    49	    },
    50	
    51	    /// Single formula write (text only — replay re-evaluates via the
    52	    /// caller's `WorkbookRuntime::recompute_all`, not via replay itself).
    53	    /// Mirrors `WorkbookRuntime::set_formula` for the persistence side.
    54	    PutFormula {
    55	        sheet: SheetId,
    56	        row: RowId,
    57	        col: ColId,
    58	        text: String,
    59	    },
    60	
    61	    /// Remove formula association at a cell (cell becomes literal-only).
    62	    /// Mirrors `Workbook::clear_formula`.
    63	    ClearFormula {
    64	        sheet: SheetId,
    65	        row: RowId,
    66	        col: ColId,
    67	    },
    68	
    69	    /// Register a defined name. Mirrors `Workbook::set_name` when
    70	    /// `scope` is `None`, and `Sheet::set_scoped_name` when `scope` is
    71	    /// `Some(sheet_id)`. **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D)** added the optional
    72	    /// `scope` field per design doc § 8.2 / Codex MEDIUM-4: a single
    73	    /// op variant covers both scopes rather than two separate ops.
    74	    ///
    75	    /// Wire compat: `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]`
    76	    /// keeps v3+old ops deserializing unchanged (missing field → `None`
    77	    /// = workbook scope, the historical behavior). Sheet-scoped names
    78	    /// emit the field; workbook-scoped names omit it.
    79	    ///
    80	    /// Replay: routes to `Workbook::set_name` (scope `None`) or
    81	    /// `Sheet::set_scoped_name` (scope `Some`); reserved-name refusal
    82	    /// surfaces as `ReplayError::NameRejected` in either path. A
    83	    /// `scope: Some(id)` referencing an unknown sheet surfaces as
    84	    /// `ReplayError::InvalidSheet`.
    85	    SetName {
    86	        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    87	        scope: Option<SheetId>,
    88	        name: String,
    89	        target: NamedTargetWire,
    90	    },
    91	
    92	    /// Create a new sheet. Mirrors `Workbook::add_sheet_with_chunk_rows`.
    93	    /// Sheet IDs are deterministic (the workbook assigns them in append
    94	    /// order); the producer doesn't pin the ID in the op, since replay
    95	    /// against an empty workbook produces the same sequence.
    96	    AddSheet { name: String, chunk_rows: u32 },
    97	
    98	    /// **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** rename an existing sheet by id. Mirrors
    99	    /// `Workbook::rename_sheet`. Carries `old_name` for replay-time
   100	    /// validation (snapshot-vs-replay reconciliation: if the snapshot
   101	    /// already has the new name, replay no-ops gracefully when current
   102	    /// canonical matches the new name). Per design doc § 3.1, the
   103	    /// producer-side runtime rewrites all stored formula text BEFORE
   104	    /// emitting this op (and emits `Op::PutFormula` ops for each
   105	    /// rewritten cell in the same BatchCommit), so replay-on-top-of-
   106	    /// snapshot OR replay-from-empty both produce correct state.
   107	    RenameSheet {
   108	        id: SheetId,
   109	        old_name: String,
   110	        new_name: String,
   111	    },
   112	
   113	    /// Register a format string at a specific id. Mirrors
   114	    /// `FormatTable::register_at`. Emitted when
   115	    /// `WorkbookRuntime::intern_format` allocates a NEW id; idempotent
   116	    /// on replay (re-registering an existing id with the same string
   117	    /// is a no-op). Re-registering at the same id with a DIFFERENT
   118	    /// string fails at `FormatTable::register_at` and surfaces as
   119	    /// `ReplayError::FormatRejected`. Shipped W5-80 (Phase 4.5.D part 4).
   120	    RegisterFormat { id: u32, string: String },
   121	
   122	    /// Set or clear a cell's format id. Mirrors
   123	    /// `CellFormatOverlay::set` (when `id` is `Some`) and `::clear`
   124	    /// (when `id` is `None`). The id MUST resolve in the workbook's
   125	    /// `FormatTable` at replay time; an unknown id surfaces as
   126	    /// `ReplayError::FormatNotRegistered`. Shipped W5-80.
   127	    SetCellFormat {
   128	        sheet: SheetId,
   129	        row: RowId,
   130	        col: ColId,
   131	        /// `None` ⇒ clear the overlay entry (cell falls back to General).
   132	        /// `Some(id)` ⇒ bind the cell to that format id.
   133	        id: Option<u32>,
   134	    },
   135	
   136	    /// One transaction's ops applied atomically at replay time. Produced
   137	    /// by `WorkbookTransaction::commit` in 2A.3.b. Replay applies each
   138	    /// inner op in order; on failure, replay reports the inner op's
   139	    /// index relative to the BatchCommit's parent index. No implicit
   140	    /// rollback at replay (commits are committed).
   141	    ///
   142	    /// Nested BatchCommits are permitted by the schema but produced
   143	    /// nowhere in production; replay handles them via recursion.
   144	    BatchCommit { ops: Vec<Op> },
   145	}

     1	//! Replay an `OpLog` against a `Workbook`.
     2	//!
     3	//! Phase 2A.3.a (2026-05-12): single entry point `replay_into(&OpLog, &mut
     4	//! Workbook, &FunctionRegistry) -> Result<usize, ReplayError>`. Returns the
     5	//! count of ops successfully applied; on failure, returns
     6	//! `ReplayError::At { index, kind }` carrying the position of the failing
     7	//! op and the underlying failure. The workbook is left in a partial state
     8	//! on failure (callers decide whether to discard, recover, or recompute).
     9	//!
    10	//! ## Replay semantics
    11	//!
    12	//! Each `Op` variant maps to one `Workbook` mutation:
    13	//!
    14	//! - `PutValue` → `Workbook::put_at` (after row/col bounds check).
    15	//! - `PutFormula` → `Workbook::put_formula` (text only; replay does NOT
    16	//!   re-evaluate — that's a separate step the caller drives via
    17	//!   `WorkbookRuntime::recompute_all`).
    18	//! - `ClearFormula` → `Workbook::clear_formula`.
    19	//! - `SetName` → `Workbook::set_name`. Reserved names (e.g. `AI`) surface
    20	//!   as `ReplayError::NameRejected`.
    21	//! - `AddSheet` → `Workbook::add_sheet_with_chunk_rows`.
    22	//! - `BatchCommit` → recurse on each inner op. Index reporting flattens:
    23	//!   a failure inside a BatchCommit reports the inner op's overall
    24	//!   position (current outer index + offset).
    25	//!
    26	//! ## Idempotence
    27	//!
    28	//! `replay_into` is idempotent only in the trivial sense: replaying twice
    29	//! against the same starting workbook produces the same final state IFF
    30	//! the underlying mutation methods are themselves idempotent (and they
    31	//! are — `put_at` / `put_formula` / `clear_formula` / `set_name` /
    32	//! `add_sheet` all are). Callers wanting at-most-once semantics across
    33	//! sessions track replay state themselves (Phase 5+ work).
    34	//!
    35	//! ## `registry` parameter
    36	//!
    37	//! Passed for API symmetry with `WorkbookRuntime::new(&mut wb, &registry)`.
    38	//! 2A.3.a's replay doesn't use it (formula evaluation is deferred to a
    39	//! separate `recompute_all` call), but keeping the parameter stable here
    40	//! avoids a breaking signature change in 2A.3.b/c when the eval path
    41	//! could land inside replay.
    42	
    43	use ql_functions::FunctionRegistry;
    44	use ql_storage::Workbook;
    45	use ql_types::{ColId, RowId, SheetId, Value, MAX_COLUMN, MAX_ROW};
    46	use thiserror::Error;
    47	
    48	use crate::log::OpLog;
    49	use crate::op::Op;
    50	
    51	/// Errors emitted by `replay_into`.
    52	#[derive(Debug, Error)]
    53	pub enum ReplayError {
    54	    /// An op-log entry failed to deserialize (corrupted log data).
    55	    #[error("replay deserialize error at op index {0}: {1}")]
    56	    Deserialize(usize, #[source] crate::error::OpLogError),
    57	
    58	    /// The op referenced an out-of-range sheet id.
    59	    #[error(
    60	        "replay invalid sheet at op index {index}: sheet {sheet} (workbook has {sheet_count})"
    61	    )]
    62	    InvalidSheet {
    63	        index: usize,
    64	        sheet: SheetId,
    65	        sheet_count: usize,
    66	    },
    67	
    68	    /// The op carried out-of-range row/col coordinates.
    69	    #[error("replay invalid cell at op index {index}: row={row} col={col} ({why})")]
    70	    InvalidCell {
    71	        index: usize,
    72	        row: RowId,
    73	        col: ColId,
    74	        why: &'static str,
    75	    },
    76	
    77	    /// Decoding the on-wire `CellWireValue` failed (e.g., unknown error sigil).
    78	    #[error("replay value-decode error at op index {index}: {source}")]
    79	    ValueDecode {
    80	        index: usize,
    81	        #[source]
    82	        source: ql_io::QbookError,
    83	    },
    84	
    85	    /// Decoding the on-wire `NamedTargetWire` failed.
    86	    #[error("replay named-target decode error at op index {index}: {source}")]
    87	    NamedTargetDecode {
    88	        index: usize,
    89	        #[source]
    90	        source: ql_io::QbookError,
    91	    },
    92	
    93	    /// `Workbook::set_name` refused the name (reserved per CORR-06).
    94	    #[error("replay name rejected at op index {index}: {name:?} ({source})")]
    95	    NameRejected {
    96	        index: usize,
    97	        name: String,
    98	        #[source]
    99	        source: ql_storage::NameTableError,
   100	    },
   101	
   102	    /// `FormatTable::register_at` refused the registration — the id is
   103	    /// already taken with a different string, or the string is already
   104	    /// at a different id. **W5-80 (Phase 4.5.D part 4).**
   105	    #[error("replay format rejected at op index {index}: {source:?}")]
   106	    FormatRejected {
   107	        index: usize,
   108	        #[source]
   109	        source: FormatRejectedSource,
   110	    },
   111	
   112	    /// `Op::SetCellFormat` referenced a format id that hasn't been
   113	    /// registered yet. The producer SHOULD always emit `RegisterFormat`
   114	    /// BEFORE `SetCellFormat` for any custom id; replay enforces that.
   115	    /// **W5-80.**
   116	    #[error("replay set-cell-format references unregistered format id {id} at op index {index}")]
   117	    FormatNotRegistered { index: usize, id: u32 },
   118	
   119	    /// **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** `Op::RenameSheet` referenced a sheet
   120	    /// whose current name matches neither `old_name` nor `new_name`
   121	    /// (i.e. the replay state has diverged from what the op recorded).
   122	    /// Surfaces a clean error rather than silently renaming a different
   123	    /// sheet.
   124	    #[error(
   125	        "replay rename-sheet name mismatch at op index {index}: sheet {id} \
   126	         expected current name {expected:?}, found {found:?}"
   127	    )]
   128	    SheetRenameNameMismatch {
   129	        index: usize,
   130	        id: SheetId,
   131	        expected: String,
   132	        found: String,
   133	    },
   134	
   135	    /// **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** `Workbook::rename_sheet` refused the
   136	    /// new name (duplicate, reserved character, empty).
   137	    #[error(
   138	        "replay rename-sheet rejected at op index {index}: sheet {id} \
   139	         from {old_name:?} to {new_name:?} ({source})"
   140	    )]
   141	    SheetRenameRejected {
   142	        index: usize,
   143	        id: SheetId,
   144	        old_name: String,
   145	        new_name: String,
   146	        #[source]
   147	        source: ql_storage::SheetNameError,
   148	    },
   149	}
   150	
   151	/// Wrapper around `ql_storage::FormatTableError` that owns its strings,
   152	/// so `ReplayError` can stay `Clone + std::error::Error` without
   153	/// borrowing into the table.
   154	#[derive(Clone, Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   155	pub enum FormatRejectedSource {
   156	    #[error("format id {id} already bound to {existing:?}, can't re-bind to {attempted:?}")]
   157	    IdCollision {
   158	        id: u32,
   159	        existing: String,
   160	        attempted: String,
   161	    },
   162	    #[error(
   163	        "format string {string:?} already at id {existing_id}, can't bind to id {attempted_id}"
   164	    )]
   165	    StringCollision {
   166	        string: String,
   167	        existing_id: u32,
   168	        attempted_id: u32,
   169	    },
   170	}
   171	
   172	impl From<ql_storage::FormatTableError> for FormatRejectedSource {
   173	    fn from(e: ql_storage::FormatTableError) -> Self {
   174	        match e {
   175	            ql_storage::FormatTableError::IdCollision {
   176	                id,
   177	                existing,
   178	                attempted,
   179	            } => FormatRejectedSource::IdCollision {
   180	                id: id.0,
   181	                existing,
   182	                attempted,
   183	            },
   184	            ql_storage::FormatTableError::StringCollision {
   185	                string,
   186	                existing_id,
   187	                attempted_id,
   188	            } => FormatRejectedSource::StringCollision {
   189	                string,
   190	                existing_id: existing_id.0,
   191	                attempted_id: attempted_id.0,
   192	            },
   193	        }
   194	    }
   195	}
   196	
   197	/// Replay every op in `log` against `workbook` in append order.
   198	///
   199	/// Returns the total count of ops applied on success. On failure, the
   200	/// workbook is in a partial state — `ReplayError::At { index, .. }` tells
   201	/// the caller how far replay got.
   202	///
   203	/// The `registry` parameter is held for API symmetry with `WorkbookRuntime`
   204	/// and is unused in 2A.3.a (replay persists formula text without
   205	/// re-evaluating; callers drive evaluation through `recompute_all`).
   206	pub fn replay_into(
   207	    log: &OpLog,
   208	    workbook: &mut Workbook,
   209	    _registry: &FunctionRegistry,
   210	) -> Result<usize, ReplayError> {
   211	    let mut count = 0;
   212	    for (index, op_result) in log.iter().enumerate() {
   213	        let op = op_result.map_err(|e| ReplayError::Deserialize(index, e))?;
   214	        apply_op(&op, workbook, index)?;
   215	        count += 1;
   216	    }
   217	    Ok(count)
   218	}
   219	
   220	/// Recursive helper. `index` is the op's position in the outer log (or
   221	/// the synthetic position of the enclosing BatchCommit for nested ops —
   222	/// 2A.3.a flattens by reporting the parent's index for nested failures).
   223	fn apply_op(op: &Op, workbook: &mut Workbook, index: usize) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   224	    match op {
   225	        Op::PutValue {
   226	            sheet,
   227	            row,
   228	            col,
   229	            value,
   230	        } => {
   231	            validate_cell(workbook, *sheet, *row, *col, index)?;
   232	            let v: Value = value
   233	                .to_value()
   234	                .map_err(|source| ReplayError::ValueDecode { index, source })?;
   235	            workbook.put_at(*sheet, *row, *col, v);
   236	            Ok(())
   237	        }
   238	        Op::PutFormula {
   239	            sheet,
   240	            row,
   241	            col,
   242	            text,
   243	        } => {
   244	            validate_cell(workbook, *sheet, *row, *col, index)?;
   245	            workbook.put_formula(*sheet, *row, *col, text.as_str());
   246	            Ok(())
   247	        }
   248	        Op::ClearFormula { sheet, row, col } => {
   249	            // clear_formula is idempotent on missing entries; we still
   250	            // validate bounds so a corrupted op-log can't sneak past.
   251	            validate_cell(workbook, *sheet, *row, *col, index)?;
   252	            workbook.clear_formula(*sheet, *row, *col);
   253	            Ok(())
   254	        }
   255	        Op::SetName {
   256	            scope,
   257	            name,
   258	            target,
   259	        } => {
   260	            let target = target
   261	                .to_target(name)
   262	                .map_err(|source| ReplayError::NamedTargetDecode { index, source })?;
   263	            match scope {
   264	                None => {
   265	                    // Workbook-scoped (historical path).
   266	                    workbook.set_name(name, target).map_err(|source| {
   267	                        ReplayError::NameRejected {
   268	                            index,
   269	                            name: name.clone(),
   270	                            source,
   271	                        }
   272	                    })?;
   273	                }
   274	                Some(sheet) => {
   275	                    // **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D):** sheet-scoped. Validate sheet
   276	                    // first so an unknown id surfaces as InvalidSheet, not
   277	                    // a panicking out-of-bounds index.
   278	                    let sheet_count = workbook.sheet_count();
   279	                    let sheet_ref =
   280	                        workbook
   281	                            .sheet_mut(*sheet)
   282	                            .ok_or(ReplayError::InvalidSheet {
   283	                                index,
   284	                                sheet: *sheet,
   285	                                sheet_count,
   286	                            })?;
   287	                    sheet_ref.set_scoped_name(name, target).map_err(|source| {
   288	                        ReplayError::NameRejected {
   289	                            index,
   290	                            name: name.clone(),
   291	                            source,
   292	                        }
   293	                    })?;
   294	                }
   295	            }
   296	            Ok(())
   297	        }
   298	        Op::AddSheet { name, chunk_rows } => {
   299	            // add_sheet panics on SheetId::MAX. Bound check would require
   300	            // running through a try_add_sheet API which doesn't exist
   301	            // (Phase 2A.6 audit M1 deferred it). For replay, we trust the
   302	            // log: a workbook that successfully produced this op originally
   303	            // had room for the sheet.
   304	            workbook.add_sheet_with_chunk_rows(name.clone(), *chunk_rows);
   305	            Ok(())
   306	        }
   307	        Op::RenameSheet {
   308	            id,
   309	            old_name,
   310	            new_name,
   311	        } => {
   312	            // **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** snapshot-vs-replay reconciliation
   313	            // per design § 3.3. Three cases:
   314	            //   1. Current sheet name == old_name → fresh rename; apply.
   315	            //   2. Current sheet name == new_name → already-renamed (likely
   316	            //      replay-on-top-of-snapshot where the snapshot captured
   317	            //      the post-rename state). No-op; success.
   318	            //   3. Current sheet name == neither → divergent state; treat as
   319	            //      InvalidSheet for the op-log's safety contract.
   320	            let current = workbook.sheet(*id).map(|s| s.name().to_owned()).ok_or(
   321	                ReplayError::InvalidSheet {
   322	                    index,
   323	                    sheet: *id,
   324	                    sheet_count: workbook.sheet_count(),
   325	                },
   326	            )?;
   327	            let canonical = |s: &str| ql_storage::Workbook::canonical_sheet_name(s);
   328	            let cur_c = canonical(&current);
   329	            let old_c = canonical(old_name);
   330	            let new_c = canonical(new_name);
   331	            if cur_c == new_c {
   332	                // Already renamed (snapshot-authoritative path). No-op.
   333	                Ok(())
   334	            } else if cur_c == old_c {
   335	                workbook
   336	                    .rename_sheet(*id, new_name.clone())
   337	                    .map_err(|source| ReplayError::SheetRenameRejected {
   338	                        index,
   339	                        id: *id,
   340	                        old_name: old_name.clone(),
   341	                        new_name: new_name.clone(),
   342	                        source,
   343	                    })?;
   344	                Ok(())
   345	            } else {
   346	                Err(ReplayError::SheetRenameNameMismatch {
   347	                    index,
   348	                    id: *id,
   349	                    expected: old_name.clone(),
   350	                    found: current,
   351	                })
   352	            }
   353	        }
   354	        Op::RegisterFormat { id, string } => {
   355	            // **W5-80:** route through `FormatTable::register_at` so
   356	            // collisions surface as `FormatRejected` (rather than the
   357	            // panic-on-duplicate behavior `intern` would have via the
   358	            // by_string fast path; `register_at` is the explicit-id form).
   359	            let fid = ql_storage::FormatId(*id);
   360	            workbook
   361	                .formats_mut()
   362	                .register_at(fid, string.as_str())
   363	                .map_err(|e| ReplayError::FormatRejected {
   364	                    index,
   365	                    source: e.into(),
   366	                })?;
   367	            Ok(())
   368	        }
   369	        Op::SetCellFormat {
   370	            sheet,
   371	            row,
   372	            col,
   373	            id,
   374	        } => {
   375	            validate_cell(workbook, *sheet, *row, *col, index)?;
   376	            // **W5-80:** require the id to be registered. Catches
   377	            // producer bugs where `SetCellFormat` was emitted without
   378	            // a preceding `RegisterFormat`. `None` means clear.
   379	            if let Some(raw_id) = id {
   380	                let fid = ql_storage::FormatId(*raw_id);
   381	                if workbook.formats().lookup(fid).is_none() {
   382	                    return Err(ReplayError::FormatNotRegistered { index, id: *raw_id });
   383	                }
   384	                workbook
   385	                    .sheet_mut(*sheet)
   386	                    .expect("sheet validated above")
   387	                    .format_overlay_mut()
   388	                    .set(*row, *col, fid);
   389	            } else {
   390	                workbook
   391	                    .sheet_mut(*sheet)
   392	                    .expect("sheet validated above")
   393	                    .format_overlay_mut()
   394	                    .clear(*row, *col);
   395	            }
   396	            Ok(())
   397	        }
   398	        Op::BatchCommit { ops } => {
   399	            for inner_op in ops {
   400	                // Phase 2A.3.a: nested ops flatten to the parent's index
   401	                // for error reporting. Phase 5+ may extend with
   402	                // (outer, inner) tuple indexing once the producer side
   403	                // emits nested commits in practice.
   404	                apply_op(inner_op, workbook, index)?;
   405	            }
   406	            Ok(())
   407	        }
   408	    }
   409	}
   410	
   411	fn validate_cell(
   412	    workbook: &Workbook,
   413	    sheet: SheetId,
   414	    row: RowId,
   415	    col: ColId,
   416	    index: usize,
   417	) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   418	    let count = workbook.sheet_count();
   419	    if (sheet as usize) >= count {
   420	        return Err(ReplayError::InvalidSheet {

   467	
   468	    /// Set a cell to a formula. Pipeline: lex → parse → bind (against `sheet`) →
   469	    /// eval against the current workbook → put_at the result + put_formula the text.
   470	    ///
   471	    /// Returns the evaluated value, or a `RuntimeError` if any pipeline stage fails.
   472	    /// On error, the workbook is unchanged (no partial writes).
   473	    ///
   474	    /// `formula_text` is the formula body without the leading `=`. The Sheet must
   475	    /// already exist.
   476	    pub fn set_formula(
   477	        &mut self,
   478	        sheet: SheetId,
   479	        row: RowId,
   480	        col: ColId,
   481	        formula_text: impl Into<Arc<str>>,
   482	    ) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
   483	        // Phase 2A.7 audit H1 (was 2A.6 L4 sheet-only): validate sheet AND
   484	        // row/col bounds up front. Without these checks, out-of-grid cells
   485	        // sneak past lex/parse/bind and panic inside `Sheet::put` (which
   486	        // asserts `row <= MAX_ROW && col <= MAX_COLUMN`).
   487	        validate_cell(self.workbook, sheet, row, col)?;
   488	
   489	        let formula_text = formula_text.into();
   490	        // Phase 2B.3: consult the bind-plan cache first. The key includes
   491	        // the current NameTable generation so a name registration since
   492	        // the last bind produces a miss → re-bind against the new table.
   493	        // Cached `Arc<ExprPlan>` is shared with `recompute_all`, so a
   494	        // subsequent recompute of this cell skips lex/parse/bind entirely.
   495	        let name_gen = self.workbook.names().generation();
   496	        let cache_key = PlanCacheKey {
   497	            text: Arc::clone(&formula_text),
   498	            sheet,
   499	            name_gen,
   500	        };
   501	        let plan: Arc<crate::plan::ExprPlan> =
   502	            self.plan_cache
   503	                .get_or_insert::<_, RuntimeError>(cache_key, || {
   504	                    let tokens = lex(formula_text.as_ref())?;
   505	                    let expr = parse(tokens)?;
   506	                    // Phase 2A.1 (2026-05-12): bind against the workbook's
   507	                    // NameTable so `Expr::NameRef` resolves against defined
   508	                    // names. W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D): pass `&Workbook` for names
   509	                    // so the two-tier sheet-then-workbook chain fires.
   510	                    Ok(bind_with_names_and_sheets(
   511	                        &expr,
   512	                        sheet,
   513	                        self.workbook,
   514	                        self.workbook,
   515	                    )?)
   516	                })?;
   517	
   518	        // Evaluate against the current workbook state. The env borrows immutably; we
   519	        // drop it before taking the mutable borrow for the write.
   520	        // Phase 3.6 (W5-39): if a calcgraph session is attached, route
   521	        // the eval through `eval_scalar_with_cache` so aggregate-range
   522	        // results land in the session's cache (next recompute on an
   523	        // unchanged range hits without re-scanning).
   524	        let value = {
   525	            let env = WorkbookEnv::new(self.workbook);
   526	            match self.graph.as_deref() {
   527	                Some(session) => crate::scalar::eval_scalar_with_cache(
   528	                    plan.as_ref(),
   529	                    &env,
   530	                    self.registry,
   531	                    session.aggregate_cache(),
   532	                ),
   533	                None => eval_scalar_with_registry(plan.as_ref(), &env, self.registry),
   534	            }
   535	        };
   536	
   537	        // Phase 2A.3.b op-log emission, BEFORE mutation. If append fails, no
   538	        // mutation; workbook stays consistent. We emit `PutFormula` only — the
   539	        // evaluated value isn't recorded because replay re-derives it via
   540	        // `WorkbookRuntime::recompute_all` (see replay.rs module docs).
   541	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
   542	            oplog.append(Op::PutFormula {
   543	                sheet,
   544	                row,
   545	                col,
   546	                text: formula_text.as_ref().to_owned(),
   547	            })?;
   548	        }
   549	
   550	        // Persist formula text + evaluated value. Phase 3.5 (CORR-25):
   551	        // a formula's output goes to the COMPUTED overlay, not the user
   552	        // overlay; if the cell previously held a user-typed value, we
   553	        // clear that lane first so the user-first read cascade doesn't
   554	        // mask the new formula output. `put_at` can't fail because
   555	        // `validate_cell` at the top of this method already rejected
   556	        // out-of-range (sheet, row, col).
   557	        self.workbook.clear_user_at(sheet, row, col);
   558	        self.workbook
   559	            .put_computed_at(sheet, row, col, value.clone());
   560	        self.workbook.put_formula(sheet, row, col, formula_text);
   561	
   562	        // Phase 3.1: notify calcgraph after the workbook mutation
   563	        // succeeds. Phase 3.2 (2026-05-12): pass the already-bound
   564	        // `ExprPlan` straight from the PlanCache so the hook walks it
   565	        // for cell/range/name + volatile deps without re-binding. The
   566	        // plan is shared by Arc; the hook only needs `&ExprPlan`.
   567	        if let Some(g) = self.graph.as_deref_mut() {
   568	            g.on_set_formula(sheet, row, col, plan.as_ref());
   569	        }
   570	
   571	        Ok(value)
   572	    }
   573	
   574	    /// Set a cell to a literal value (no formula). Clears any existing formula
   575	    /// association at the cell — typing a value over a formula cell deletes the
   576	    /// formula per Excel canon. Phase 2A.7 audit H1: validates sheet + row/col
   577	    /// bounds; returns `RuntimeError` instead of panicking on out-of-grid input.
   578	    pub fn set_value(
   579	        &mut self,
   580	        sheet: SheetId,
   581	        row: RowId,
   582	        col: ColId,
   583	        value: Value,
   584	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   585	        validate_cell(self.workbook, sheet, row, col)?;
   586	
   587	        // Phase 2A.3.b: emit ops BEFORE mutating, so a serialization failure
   588	        // (NaN/Inf) leaves the workbook unchanged. `CellWireValue::from_value`
   589	        // returns None for `Value::Blank`; we skip the `PutValue` in that case
   590	        // (documented limitation — replaying a Blank write won't reset a
   591	        // prior non-Blank value; rare enough that the wire-format expansion
   592	        // is deferred to Phase 5+). The `ClearFormula` still fires below if
   593	        // the cell had a formula, so the produced log captures the formula
   594	        // removal even when the literal value is Blank.
   595	        //
   596	        // Phase 2B.7 audit H2 (2026-05-12): when BOTH PutValue and
   597	        // ClearFormula need to land, wrap them in a single `Op::BatchCommit`
   598	        // so the pair is atomic at the Loro level — partial-pair failure
   599	        // (first appended, second fails) is now impossible.
   600	        let had_formula = self.workbook.formula_at(sheet, row, col).is_some();
   601	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
   602	            let mut log_ops: Vec<Op> = Vec::with_capacity(2);
   603	            if let Some(wire) = CellWireValue::from_value(&value) {
   604	                log_ops.push(Op::PutValue {
   605	                    sheet,
   606	                    row,
   607	                    col,
   608	                    value: wire,
   609	                });
   610	            }
   611	            if had_formula {
   612	                log_ops.push(Op::ClearFormula { sheet, row, col });
   613	            }
   614	            match log_ops.len() {
   615	                0 => {} // Blank value on a non-formula cell — no-op.
   616	                1 => oplog.append(log_ops.into_iter().next().unwrap())?,
   617	                _ => oplog.append(Op::BatchCommit { ops: log_ops })?,
   618	            }
   619	        }
   620	
   621	        self.workbook.put_at(sheet, row, col, value);
   622	        self.workbook.clear_formula(sheet, row, col);
   623	
   624	        // Phase 3.1: notify calcgraph. `set_value` always fires
   625	        // `on_set_value`; if the cell had a formula that we just cleared,
   626	        // also fires `on_clear_formula` so the graph can detach old deps
   627	        // when Phase 3.3 wires that path.
   628	        if let Some(g) = self.graph.as_deref_mut() {
   629	            g.on_set_value(sheet, row, col);
   630	            if had_formula {
   631	                g.on_clear_formula(sheet, row, col);
   632	            }
   633	        }
   634	
   635	        Ok(())
   636	    }
   637	
   638	    /// Phase 2B.5 (2026-05-12): register a defined name through the runtime,
   639	    /// emitting `Op::SetName` into the attached op log (if any). This is the
   640	    /// op-log-recording wrapper for `Workbook::set_name`; product code SHOULD
   641	    /// route through here so the mutation lands in the op log.
   642	    ///
   643	    /// Direct callers of `Workbook::set_name` bypass the op log silently —
   644	    /// that path is documented as low-level and intended only for tests, the
   645	    /// qbook loader (where the workbook is being constructed from disk and
   646	    /// op-log history is loaded separately), and other engine-internal
   647	    /// reconstruction code. See GAP-O-01 in `docs/known-gaps.md`.
   648	    pub fn set_name(
   649	        &mut self,
   650	        name: &str,
   651	        target: ql_storage::NamedTarget,
   652	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   653	        // Phase 2B.7 audit H3 (was 2B.5 mutate-first): validate → append →
   654	        // mutate so neither failure mode leaves engine state divergent:
   655	        //
   656	        //   1. Reserved-name rejection: caught by `NameTable::would_accept`
   657	        //      before anything else runs. Workbook unmodified, log unmodified.
   658	        //   2. Op-log append failure: caught BEFORE the workbook mutation.
   659	        //      Workbook still unmodified, log unmodified.
   660	        //
   661	        // The prior mutate-first ordering left a divergence window where
   662	        // the workbook had the name but the log didn't — see audit H3 for
   663	        // why that was wrong. The append-first ordering used by set_value /
   664	        // set_formula / clear_formula / add_sheet now extends here.
   665	        //
   666	        // Wire-form encoding canonicalizes the name to upper case to match
   667	        // `NameTable::set`'s on-write canonicalization, so the recorded
   668	        // form is stable regardless of how the caller cased the name.
   669	        self.workbook.names().would_accept(name)?;
   670	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
   671	            let target_wire = ql_io::NamedTargetWire::from_target(&target);
   672	            oplog.append(Op::SetName {
   673	                scope: None,
   674	                name: name.to_ascii_uppercase(),
   675	                target: target_wire,
   676	            })?;
   677	        }
   678	        // Now the mutation cannot fail (reserved-name already pre-checked).
   679	        // `set_name` returns Result for forward-compat with future
   680	        // NameTableError variants; expect them to be pre-checkable via
   681	        // `would_accept`.
   682	        self.workbook.set_name(name, target)?;
   683	
   684	        // Phase 3.1: notify calcgraph. Today a counter-bump; Phase 3.3
   685	        // will mark all formulas containing this name dirty.
   686	        if let Some(g) = self.graph.as_deref_mut() {
   687	            g.on_set_name(name);
   688	        }
   689	
   690	        Ok(())
   691	    }
   692	
   693	    /// **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D):** register a sheet-scoped defined name
   694	    /// through the runtime, emitting `Op::SetName { scope: Some(sheet), .. }`
   695	    /// into the attached op log (if any). Sheet-scoped names shadow
   696	    /// workbook-scoped names with the same identifier when accessed
   697	    /// from a formula on `sheet`, per Excel canon (XS-4-03).
   698	    ///
   699	    /// Validation matches `set_name`:
   700	    /// - `Workbook::sheet(sheet)` must exist; otherwise
   701	    ///   `RuntimeError::InvalidSheet`.
   702	    /// - Reserved-name guard fires (currently `AI` per CORR-06); the
   703	    ///   reserved set is workbook-global, so sheet-scoped names are
   704	    ///   refused with the same rule.
   705	    /// - Op-log append failure fails BEFORE the workbook mutation so
   706	    ///   neither failure mode leaves engine state divergent.
   707	    pub fn set_sheet_scoped_name(
   708	        &mut self,
   709	        sheet: SheetId,
   710	        name: &str,
   711	        target: ql_storage::NamedTarget,
   712	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   713	        // 1. Validate sheet id exists.
   714	        let sheet_count = self.workbook.sheet_count();
   715	        if self.workbook.sheet(sheet).is_none() {
   716	            return Err(RuntimeError::InvalidSheet { sheet, sheet_count });
   717	        }
   718	        // 2. Pre-check reserved-name guard so a rejection doesn't
   719	        //    leave a phantom op-log entry. We use the workbook's
   720	        //    `NameTable::would_accept` since the reserved-name set
   721	        //    is workbook-global (per is_reserved_name in storage).
   722	        self.workbook.names().would_accept(name)?;
   723	        // 3. Append op-log entry BEFORE mutation.
   724	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
   725	            let target_wire = ql_io::NamedTargetWire::from_target(&target);
   726	            oplog.append(Op::SetName {
   727	                scope: Some(sheet),
   728	                name: name.to_ascii_uppercase(),
   729	                target: target_wire,
   730	            })?;
   731	        }
   732	        // 4. Mutate. validation already passed; `set_scoped_name`
   733	        //    returns Result for forward-compat.
   734	        self.workbook
   735	            .sheet_mut(sheet)
   736	            .expect("sheet existence already validated")
   737	            .set_scoped_name(name, target)?;
   738	
   739	        // 5. Calcgraph notification — fan out the same as workbook-
   740	        //    scoped sets. Phase 3.3's name→formula tracking is name-
   741	        //    keyed and doesn't currently distinguish scopes; over-
   742	        //    invalidation is the conservative direction here.
   743	        if let Some(g) = self.graph.as_deref_mut() {
   744	            g.on_set_name(name);
   745	        }
   746	
   747	        Ok(())
   748	    }
   749	
   750	    /// Phase 2B.5 (2026-05-12): append a new sheet through the runtime,
   751	    /// emitting `Op::AddSheet` into the attached op log (if any). Returns
   752	    /// the new sheet's `SheetId`. Wraps `Workbook::add_sheet_with_chunk_rows`.
   753	    ///
   754	    /// `chunk_rows` is the per-sheet chunk size. Pass `16_384` (the engine
   755	    /// default) unless you have a specific reason — the qbook loader uses
   756	    /// the saved chunk_rows so layouts round-trip.
   757	    ///
   758	    /// Direct callers of `Workbook::add_sheet` / `add_sheet_with_chunk_rows`
   759	    /// bypass the op log silently — that path is documented as low-level.
   760	    /// See GAP-O-02 in `docs/known-gaps.md`.
   761	    pub fn add_sheet(
   762	        &mut self,
   763	        name: impl Into<String>,
   764	        chunk_rows: u32,
   765	    ) -> Result<SheetId, RuntimeError> {
   766	        // Phase 2B.7 audit H1: pre-validate inputs BEFORE the op-log append
   767	        // so a bad value can't leave a phantom AddSheet in the log followed
   768	        // by a process-killing panic from `ColumnStore::with_chunk_rows` or
   769	        // `Workbook::add_sheet_with_chunk_rows` (assert on `id >= SheetId::MAX`).
   770	        if chunk_rows == 0 {
   771	            return Err(RuntimeError::InvalidChunkRows(chunk_rows));
   772	        }
   773	        let current_sheet_count = self.workbook.sheet_count() as u32;
   774	        if current_sheet_count >= SheetId::MAX as u32 {
   775	            return Err(RuntimeError::TooManySheets {
   776	                current: current_sheet_count,
   777	                max: SheetId::MAX as u32,
   778	            });
   779	        }
   780	
   781	        let name: String = name.into();
   782	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
   783	            oplog.append(Op::AddSheet {
   784	                name: name.clone(),
   785	                chunk_rows,
   786	            })?;
   787	        }
   788	        let new_id = self.workbook.add_sheet_with_chunk_rows(name, chunk_rows);
   789	
   790	        // Phase 3.1: notify calcgraph. Today a counter-bump; Phase 4.6
   791	        // will use this to track per-sheet structure generations for
   792	        // cross-sheet reference invalidation.
   793	        if let Some(g) = self.graph.as_deref_mut() {
   794	            g.on_add_sheet(new_id);
   795	        }
   796	
   797	        Ok(new_id)
   798	    }
   799	
   800	    /// **W5-91 (Phase 4.6.C):** rename a sheet by id. Walks every
   801	    /// stored formula in `formula_cells` and every `NamedTarget::Formula`
   802	    /// entry, rewrites cross-sheet references to the new sheet name via
   803	    /// `lex → parse → rewrite → print`, emits the rewritten text + the
   804	    /// `Op::RenameSheet` op atomically (single `BatchCommit`), applies
   805	    /// the rewrites to storage, then swaps `Sheet::name`. Bumps the
   806	    /// `PlanCache` generation so cached bound plans miss on the next
   807	    /// recompute.
   808	    ///
   809	    /// Validation: rejects empty / Excel-reserved-char / canonical-
   810	    /// duplicate new names via `Workbook::validate_sheet_name`.
   811	    ///
   812	    /// **Tokenization-aware:** the parse + rewrite + print round-trip
   813	    /// catches every sheet-qualified ref in the AST, including those
   814	    /// inside string literals' position (it doesn't actually substitute
   815	    /// inside `"..."` because the parser keeps those as `Expr::String`).
   816	    /// If a formula's text fails to lex/parse (corrupt input from a
   817	    /// historical bug), the text is left UNCHANGED — defensive against
   818	    /// breaking otherwise-recoverable workbooks at rename time.
   819	    ///
   820	    /// Direct callers of `Workbook::rename_sheet` bypass formula-text
   821	    /// rewriting + op-log recording — that path is loader-only.
   822	    pub fn rename_sheet(
   823	        &mut self,
   824	        id: SheetId,
   825	        new_name: impl Into<String>,
   826	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   827	        let new_name: String = new_name.into();
   828	        // Capture old name BEFORE any mutation (defensive — if anything
   829	        // below errors, the workbook stays as-is).
   830	        let old_name = self.workbook.sheet(id).map(|s| s.name().to_owned()).ok_or(
   831	            RuntimeError::InvalidSheet {
   832	                sheet: id,
   833	                sheet_count: self.workbook.sheet_count(),
   834	            },
   835	        )?;
   836	        // Validate new name. Skip the validation if canonical name is
   837	        // unchanged (case-only rename is allowed); `Workbook::rename_sheet`
   838	        // will apply the display update.
   839	        let old_canonical = ql_storage::Workbook::canonical_sheet_name(&old_name);
   840	        let new_canonical = ql_storage::Workbook::canonical_sheet_name(&new_name);
   841	        if new_canonical != old_canonical {
   842	            self.workbook.validate_sheet_name(&new_name)?;
   843	        }
   844	        // Rewrite stored formula text. Collect rewrites into a batch so
   845	        // the op log records them alongside the rename atomically.
   846	        let new_name_arc: std::sync::Arc<str> = std::sync::Arc::from(new_name.as_str());
   847	        let mut rewritten_cells: Vec<(SheetId, RowId, ColId, String)> = Vec::new();
   848	        for (sheet, row, col, text) in self.workbook.iter_formulas() {
   849	            if let Some(new_text) =
   850	                rewrite_formula_text_for_sheet_rename(text.as_ref(), &old_canonical, &new_name_arc)
   851	            {
   852	                rewritten_cells.push((sheet, row, col, new_text));
   853	            }
   854	        }
   855	        // (Phase 4.6.D / future): walk NamedTarget::Formula entries for
   856	        // the same rewrite. Today named formulas are deferred so
   857	        // formula_cells is the only text-bearing surface to rewrite.
   858	
   859	        // Append-before-mutate: emit the BatchCommit op first so a
   860	        // failing append leaves storage unchanged. The batch carries:
   861	        //   [PutFormula(cell, new_text)] × N  +  RenameSheet
   862	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
   863	            let mut ops: Vec<Op> = Vec::with_capacity(rewritten_cells.len() + 1);
   864	            for (s, r, c, ref new_text) in &rewritten_cells {
   865	                ops.push(Op::PutFormula {
   866	                    sheet: *s,
   867	                    row: *r,
   868	                    col: *c,
   869	                    text: new_text.clone(),
   870	                });
   871	            }
   872	            ops.push(Op::RenameSheet {
   873	                id,
   874	                old_name: old_name.clone(),
   875	                new_name: new_name.clone(),
   876	            });
   877	            oplog.append(Op::BatchCommit { ops })?;
   878	        }
   879	        // Apply mutations. Rewrites go in first so the storage layer
   880	        // never sees a state where the sheet name is new but formula
   881	        // text still references the old name. (Order matters only for
   882	        // observers that race — single-threaded mutation doesn't care,
   883	        // but the invariant is cleaner.)
   884	        for (s, r, c, new_text) in rewritten_cells {
   885	            self.workbook.put_formula(s, r, c, new_text.as_str());
   886	        }
   887	        // Swap the name. Cannot fail here — validation already passed.
   888	        self.workbook
   889	            .rename_sheet(id, new_name)
   890	            .expect("rename_sheet validation already passed");
   891	        // Wipe the PlanCache so cached bound plans (which baked in the
   892	        // old SheetId via SheetRef::Name resolution) miss on next
   893	        // recompute and rebind against the new sheet name. Sheet
   894	        // rename is edit-rate, not recompute-rate; a full cache flush
   895	        // is acceptable per design § 10.5 / Codex MEDIUM-7. (A
   896	        // finer-grained `sheet_gen` counter could be wired in a follow-
   897	        // up — filed as a future polish item.)
   898	        self.plan_cache.clear();
   899	        Ok(())
   900	    }
   901	
   902	    /// Phase 2B.5 (2026-05-12): clear a cell's formula association through
   903	    /// the runtime, emitting `Op::ClearFormula` into the attached op log (if
   904	    /// any). Idempotent: clearing a cell with no formula is a no-op for the
   905	    /// workbook AND for the op log (no entry emitted) — avoiding spurious
   906	    /// "removed nothing" entries.
   907	    ///
   908	    /// Note: `set_value` already emits `ClearFormula` automatically when it
   909	    /// overwrites a formula cell. This method is for callers that want to
   910	    /// explicitly strip a formula without changing the cell's value.
   911	    ///
   912	    /// Direct callers of `Workbook::clear_formula` bypass the op log
   913	    /// silently. See GAP-O-03 in `docs/known-gaps.md`.
   914	    pub fn clear_formula(
   915	        &mut self,
   916	        sheet: SheetId,
   917	        row: RowId,
   918	        col: ColId,
   919	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   920	        validate_cell(self.workbook, sheet, row, col)?;
   921	        let had_formula = self.workbook.formula_at(sheet, row, col).is_some();
   922	        if !had_formula {
   923	            // No-op: nothing to record, nothing to mutate.
   924	            return Ok(());
   925	        }
   926	        // Producer-replay equivalence: `Workbook::clear_formula` only strips
   927	        // the formula text. Phase 3.5 (CORR-25) made it also drop the
   928	        // computed-overlay entry (so the cell's prior formula output
   929	        // doesn't linger uncoupled from a formula). To preserve the
   930	        // "strip formula, keep value" semantic, we explicitly move the
   931	        // current value into the user-overlay BEFORE clearing — Excel's
   932	        // canonical interpretation is "convert formula to literal," and
   933	        // a literal value lives on the user lane. The op-log path records
   934	        // PutValue + ClearFormula so replay performs the same
   935	        // user-overlay write + clear pair.
   936	        //
   937	        // Order matters: write user BEFORE clear_formula. Otherwise the
   938	        // computed entry vanishes first and `current_value` would already
   939	        // be Blank.
   940	        //

exec
/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba crates/ql-io/src/qbook_format.rs | sed -n '1,360p'" in /Users/sanzhar/Documents/Sanzhar/Sanzhar/quantlab/quantlab-quantbook/quantbook-engine
