     1	//! `.qbook/` workbook directory format.
     2	//!
     3	//! Per spec Part V §3 + T2-D01 (Round 7 architectural lock). The on-disk shape:
     4	//!
     5	//! ```text
     6	//! my-workbook.qbook/
     7	//! ├── workbook.toml          # envelope: schema_version, name, sheet list, names
     8	//! └── sheets/
     9	//!     ├── 0.jsonl            # sheet 0 cells, one JSON line per non-blank cell
    10	//!     ├── 1.jsonl
    11	//!     └── ...
    12	//! ```
    13	//!
    14	//! - **TOML envelope** (`workbook.toml`): human-readable metadata, sheet manifest,
    15	//!   defined-names table (since v2). Users can edit it by hand if needed.
    16	//! - **JSONL per sheet**: one cell per line, append-friendly, line-oriented (clean
    17	//!   diffs in tools that don't know about the format). Blank cells without formulas
    18	//!   are NOT emitted — the file is a sparse representation.
    19	//! - **Per-sheet partitioning**: opening one sheet doesn't require parsing all
    20	//!   others. Scales to multi-million-cell workbooks at the partition boundary.
    21	//!
    22	//! ## Schema versions
    23	//!
    24	//! - **v1** (Phase 1 W5-6 + W5-9): envelope { schema_version, name, sheets },
    25	//!   CellRecord { row, col, value, formula? }. CellWireValue ∈ {Number, Boolean,
    26	//!   Text, Error}.
    27	//! - **v2** (Phase 2A.8, this revision; megaudit closure for H2/M8-M12):
    28	//!     - Envelope gains `names: Option<NamesSection>` for `NameTable` persistence
    29	//!       (closes M12). v1 files load as `names: None`.
    30	//!     - `CellWireValue::Pending` variant for formula-bearing cells whose saved
    31	//!       value would be `Value::Blank` (closes M11 — was previously encoded as
    32	//!       `Error(#NULL!)` which conflated a real spreadsheet error with a
    33	//!       not-yet-evaluated formula).
    34	//!     - `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` on every envelope / record / wire-value
    35	//!       struct (closes M9 — previously unknown fields silently dropped on read).
    36	//!
    37	//! ## Compatibility policy
    38	//!
    39	//! - **v1 → v2 load**: ACCEPTED. The v2 reader treats a v1 envelope as
    40	//!   `names: None` and converts legacy `Error(#NULL!)` placeholders into Pending
    41	//!   when the cell carries a formula. Existing v1 fixtures continue to load.
    42	//! - **v2 → v1 load**: REJECTED via the existing `UnsupportedSchema` path. No
    43	//!   silent forward-compat per the no-fallbacks rule.
    44	//! - **Within a version**: `deny_unknown_fields` rejects extra fields loudly.
    45	//!   Any future additive change requires an explicit version bump.
    46	//!
    47	//! ## Atomic save (Phase 2A.8 megaudit H2 closure)
    48	//!
    49	//! Save protocol uses a backup-rename sequence so a crash at any moment leaves
    50	//! either the prior workbook or the new one intact at `path` (or recoverable
    51	//! from a `.bak-<random>` sibling):
    52	//!
    53	//! ```text
    54	//! 1. write_workbook_to_dir(wb, name, &temp)     // temp = <path>.tmp-save-<random>
    55	//! 2. if path.exists(): fs::rename(path, &bak)   // bak = <path>.bak-<random>
    56	//! 3. fs::rename(&temp, path)                    // install new
    57	//! 4. fs::remove_dir_all(&bak)                   // best-effort cleanup (logged on failure)
    58	//! ```
    59	//!
    60	//! Invariant: between any two adjacent steps, **at least one** of `path` or
    61	//! `bak` contains a complete valid workbook.
    62	//!
    63	//! `load_workbook` runs `recover_from_crashed_save` first, which detects
    64	//! orphan `.bak-*` siblings and either rolls forward (cleanup if `path` is
    65	//! valid) or rolls back (rename `bak` → `path` if `path` is missing/invalid).
    66	//!
    67	//! ## Deferred to Phase 3+
    68	//! - Computed-overlay separation (CORR-25 deferred).
    69	//! - Cell formatting / number formats / styles.
    70	//! - Sheet-scoped named ranges (`Sheet1!Local`).
    71	
    72	use std::fs;
    73	use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, BufWriter, Write};
    74	use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
    75	
    76	use ql_storage::{NamedTarget, Sheet, Workbook};
    77	use ql_types::{Address, ColId, ErrorValue, Range, RowId, SheetId, Value, MAX_COLUMN, MAX_ROW};
    78	use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
    79	use thiserror::Error;
    80	
    81	/// On-disk schema version. Bumped on incompatible changes.
    82	///
    83	/// - `1` = Phase 1 W5-6 + W5-9: cell values + optional formulas.
    84	/// - `2` = Phase 2A.8 megaudit closure: adds NameTable persistence, explicit
    85	///   `Pending` CellWireValue, `deny_unknown_fields` strictness.
    86	///
    87	/// Loaders accept v1 AND v2 envelopes (v2 reader rewrites v1's `Error(#NULL!)`
    88	/// placeholders into `Pending` when the cell carries a formula). v1 readers
    89	/// (pre-2A.8 binaries, if any exist) refuse v2 via the `UnsupportedSchema`
    90	/// error path.
    91	/// **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** bumped to v3. v3 adds an optional
    92	/// `date_system` envelope field (`"1900"` or `"1904"`, default `"1900"`
    93	/// on missing). Loaders accept v1, v2, AND v3 envelopes. A v2 envelope
    94	/// loaded by a v3 reader gets `Workbook::date_system = Excel1900` (the
    95	/// default). A v3 envelope's `date_system` is currently always set on
    96	/// save; old binaries (v2 readers) refuse v3 via `UnsupportedSchema`.
    97	///
    98	/// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** bumped to v4. v4 adds:
    99	///   - top-level `formats: Option<FormatsSection>` — custom format
   100	///     entries (id >= 164). Excel built-in ids 0-163 are NOT persisted;
   101	///     `FormatTable::default()` re-seeds them on load.
   102	///   - per-sheet `format_overlay: Option<Vec<FormatOverlayEntry>>` —
   103	///     sparse `(row, col, id)` tuples binding cells to format ids.
   104	///
   105	/// **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D):** bumped to v5. v5 adds:
   106	///   - `NamedEntry.scope: Option<u16>` — `None` = workbook-scoped
   107	///     (existing behavior; v1-v4 default), `Some(id)` = sheet-scoped
   108	///     (new). The field is `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = ...)]`
   109	///     so v1-v4 wire payloads continue to deserialize cleanly with
   110	///     `scope = None` on every entry.
   111	///
   112	/// Loaders accept v1..=v5. v1-v4 envelopes load with all-workbook-
   113	/// scoped names (regression-neutral). v4 readers refuse v5 via
   114	/// `UnsupportedSchema` (the schema_version comparison upgrade is the
   115	/// only fail-loud surface — `NamedEntry` itself uses `serde(default)`
   116	/// so a v4 reader that somehow saw a v5 `NamedEntry` with `scope: Some`
   117	/// would deserialize but then mis-route the name to the workbook scope
   118	/// — the version gate prevents that path).
   119	pub const WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 5;
   120	
   121	/// The earliest schema version this reader still accepts. v1 fixtures (Phase 1)
   122	/// continue to load on v2 binaries; older versions would need explicit handling.
   123	///
   124	/// Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 LOW-9: now `pub` so external callers (e.g.
   125	/// `ql-oplog`) can inspect the accepted version range without reaching into
   126	/// qbook_format internals.
   127	pub const MIN_SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 1;
   128	
   129	/// Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 H1: sentinel filename written inside every
   130	/// engine-saved workbook directory. Recovery scanning uses this to
   131	/// distinguish engine-owned `.bak-<random>` siblings (which it may delete
   132	/// or restore) from user-created sibling directories that happen to match
   133	/// the `.bak-*` pattern (e.g., `book.qbook.bak-2025-review`).
   134	///
   135	/// The marker is written LAST in `write_workbook_to_dir` (after envelope +
   136	/// all sheet JSONLs), so a partial write doesn't leave a misleading marker
   137	/// in an incomplete directory. A v1 workbook re-saved on a v2 binary gains
   138	/// the marker after one successful save; until then, recovery will refuse
   139	/// to touch marker-less `.bak-*` siblings.
   140	const ATOMIC_SAVE_MARKER_FILENAME: &str = ".atomic-save-marker-v1";
   141	
   142	/// Errors that can occur during save / load.
   143	#[derive(Debug, Error)]
   144	pub enum QbookError {
   145	    #[error("I/O error: {0}")]
   146	    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
   147	
   148	    #[error("TOML deserialize error: {0}")]
   149	    TomlDe(#[from] toml::de::Error),
   150	
   151	    #[error("TOML serialize error: {0}")]
   152	    TomlSer(#[from] toml::ser::Error),
   153	
   154	    #[error("JSON error: {0}")]
   155	    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),
   156	
   157	    #[error(
   158	        "unsupported schema version {found} (this build supports versions \
   159	         {MIN_SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION}..={WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION})"
   160	    )]
   161	    UnsupportedSchema { found: u32 },
   162	
   163	    /// Phase 2A.8 audit M12: a NamedTargetWire couldn't be reconstructed into a
   164	    /// `ql_storage::NamedTarget`. Carries the name and a reason string.
   165	    #[error("malformed name {name:?}: {reason}")]
   166	    MalformedName { name: String, reason: String },
   167	
   168	    /// Phase 2A.8 audit H2: `path` doesn't have a parent directory or sensible
   169	    /// file name, so the atomic-save temp/backup paths can't be derived.
   170	    /// Surfaces from `make_save_paths` rather than the prior `unwrap_or` silent
   171	    /// fallback.
   172	    #[error("invalid workbook path {path:?}: {reason}")]
   173	    InvalidPath { path: PathBuf, reason: &'static str },
   174	
   175	    /// Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 M5: step 3 of the atomic-save protocol
   176	    /// (rename temp → target) failed AND the rollback (rename backup → target)
   177	    /// also failed. The user-visible state is "workbook may now be unavailable
   178	    /// at `path`; backup data may still be at `backup_path`." Surfaces this
   179	    /// fact directly instead of just bubbling up the original temp-rename
   180	    /// error and burying the rollback failure in stderr.
   181	    #[error(
   182	        "atomic save rollback failed at {backup_path:?}: install error ({install}); \
   183	         backup restore error ({restore}). Workbook may be unavailable at the target; \
   184	         the backup directory still holds the prior state."
   185	    )]
   186	    AtomicSaveRollbackFailed {
   187	        backup_path: PathBuf,
   188	        install: String,
   189	        restore: String,
   190	    },
   191	
   192	    #[error("malformed cell record in {file:?} at line {line}: {detail}")]
   193	    MalformedCell {
   194	        file: PathBuf,
   195	        line: usize,
   196	        detail: String,
   197	    },
   198	
   199	    #[error("workbook directory {path:?} does not exist or is not a directory")]
   200	    NotADirectory { path: PathBuf },
   201	
   202	    /// A cell value that doesn't survive JSON round-trip cleanly. serde_json
   203	    /// silently encodes NaN/Inf as `null`, which then fails to load as a Number.
   204	    /// Save-side validation rejects them with this error. Audit M6 fix (2026-05-12).
   205	    #[error(
   206	        "non-finite f64 cell at sheet {sheet} row {row} col {col}: {value} cannot be serialized"
   207	    )]
   208	    NonFiniteNumber {
   209	        sheet: SheetId,
   210	        row: RowId,
   211	        col: ColId,
   212	        value: f64,
   213	    },
   214	
   215	    #[error("missing required file {file:?}")]
   216	    MissingFile { file: PathBuf },
   217	
   218	    /// Sheet ids in the envelope must be sequential 0..N. Audit M3 fix (2026-05-12).
   219	    #[error("non-sequential sheet ids in envelope: expected id {expected}, found {found}")]
   220	    NonSequentialSheetIds { expected: u16, found: u16 },
   221	
   222	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** envelope's `formats` section
   223	    /// references an id whose registration collides with the
   224	    /// pre-populated built-in table OR an internal inconsistency
   225	    /// (same string at two ids). The `details` field is the
   226	    /// `FormatTableError`'s `Debug` rendering.
   227	    #[error("malformed format entry id={id}: {details}")]
   228	    MalformedFormat { id: u32, details: String },
   229	
   230	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** per-sheet `format_overlay` carries
   231	    /// coordinates outside the workbook's row/col bounds.
   232	    #[error("malformed format overlay entry on sheet {sheet}: row={row}, col={col} ({why})")]
   233	    MalformedFormatOverlay {
   234	        sheet: u16,
   235	        row: u32,
   236	        col: u32,
   237	        why: &'static str,
   238	    },
   239	}
   240	
   241	/// TOML envelope for the workbook. Top-level metadata.
   242	///
   243	/// Phase 2A.8: `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` rejects any unknown TOML key —
   244	/// previously the loader silently dropped extras, which would let a v2-only
   245	/// field be skipped by a still-v1 reader. Any future field requires an
   246	/// explicit schema-version bump.
   247	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   248	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   249	pub struct WorkbookEnvelope {
   250	    pub schema_version: u32,
   251	    /// User-supplied workbook name. Defaults to the directory name when omitted.
   252	    pub name: String,
   253	    /// Per-sheet metadata in id order.
   254	    pub sheets: Vec<SheetEnvelope>,
   255	    /// Phase 2A.8: workbook-scope defined-names section. Present in v2 envelopes
   256	    /// (`Some` even when empty), absent in v1. `#[serde(default)]` lets v1 files
   257	    /// load with `names: None`; the loader treats that as "no names registered."
   258	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   259	    pub names: Option<NamesSection>,
   260	    /// **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** Excel date system. Present in v3 envelopes
   261	    /// (always `Some` on save), absent in v1/v2 (loader maps `None` to
   262	    /// `DateSystem::Excel1900` — the default).
   263	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   264	    pub date_system: Option<DateSystemWire>,
   265	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** workbook-level format-string
   266	    /// interning table. v4 envelopes carry custom format entries (id ≥
   267	    /// 164) only; built-ins 0-163 are re-seeded by `FormatTable::default()`
   268	    /// on load. v1-v3 envelopes omit the field; the loader treats `None`
   269	    /// as "no custom formats registered" and proceeds with defaults.
   270	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   271	    pub formats: Option<FormatsSection>,
   272	}
   273	
   274	/// **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** wire representation of `ql_types::DateSystem`.
   275	/// Serialized as a string `"1900"` or `"1904"` for human-readable TOML.
   276	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   277	pub enum DateSystemWire {
   278	    #[serde(rename = "1900")]
   279	    Excel1900,
   280	    #[serde(rename = "1904")]
   281	    Excel1904,
   282	}
   283	
   284	impl DateSystemWire {
   285	    pub fn from_runtime(system: ql_types::DateSystem) -> Self {
   286	        match system {
   287	            ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1900 => Self::Excel1900,
   288	            ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1904 => Self::Excel1904,
   289	        }
   290	    }
   291	
   292	    pub fn to_runtime(self) -> ql_types::DateSystem {
   293	        match self {
   294	            Self::Excel1900 => ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1900,
   295	            Self::Excel1904 => ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1904,
   296	        }
   297	    }
   298	}
   299	
   300	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   301	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   302	pub struct SheetEnvelope {
   303	    pub id: u16,
   304	    pub name: String,
   305	    /// Per-sheet chunk size (default 16384). Phase 1 W5-6 captures the constructed
   306	    /// value so loaders reconstruct sheets at the same chunk layout.
   307	    pub chunk_rows: u32,
   308	    /// Highest-row written + 1, conservative. Reads beyond this still return Blank;
   309	    /// the field exists for save-side allocation hints and observability.
   310	    pub row_extent: u32,
   311	    pub col_extent: u32,
   312	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** per-sheet sparse cell-format overlay.
   313	    /// `Some([])` and `None` are equivalent ("no custom formats on this
   314	    /// sheet"); save serializes as `None` for v4 sheets with empty
   315	    /// overlay to keep TOML minimal. Loader maps `None` → empty overlay.
   316	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   317	    pub format_overlay: Option<Vec<FormatOverlayEntry>>,
   318	}
   319	
   320	/// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** workbook-level format-table wire format.
   321	///
   322	/// Persists ONLY custom entries (`id >= FIRST_CUSTOM_FORMAT_ID`). Built-in
   323	/// ids 0-163 are re-seeded by `FormatTable::default()` at load time, so
   324	/// putting them on disk would bloat every workbook with the same constants.
   325	/// Entries are sorted by id for deterministic diffs.
   326	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   327	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   328	pub struct FormatsSection {
   329	    pub entries: Vec<FormatEntry>,
   330	}
   331	
   332	/// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** one row in the workbook FormatTable.
   333	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   334	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   335	pub struct FormatEntry {
   336	    pub id: u32,
   337	    pub string: String,
   338	}
   339	
   340	/// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** one entry in a sheet's cell-format
   341	/// overlay — `(row, col)` ↦ FormatId. Sorted by `(row, col)` on save
   342	/// for deterministic diffs.
   343	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   344	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   345	pub struct FormatOverlayEntry {
   346	    pub row: u32,
   347	    pub col: u32,
   348	    pub id: u32,
   349	}
   350	
   351	/// Phase 2A.8: workbook-scope defined names. The on-disk wire format mirrors
   352	/// `ql_storage::NameTable` + `NamedTarget`, projected into serializable shapes.
   353	///
   354	/// Names are stored in canonical (upper-case) form, matching `NameTable::set`'s
   355	/// canonicalization. Order is sorted ascending by name for deterministic diffs.
   356	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   357	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   358	pub struct NamesSection {
   359	    pub entries: Vec<NamedEntry>,
   360	}

    73	| GAP-F-06 | `COUNT` provenance-aware semantics not implemented. Excel canon: `COUNT(TRUE, "1")` direct args = 2 (counts logical + numeric text); `COUNT(A1:A2)` over the same cells = 0 (skips them as range/ref). Quantbook's eval dispatcher pre-evaluates all args before calling the function body, so `count()` cannot distinguish provenance — always skips text/bool. Filed W5-67 closure (Codex Phase 4.4 mega-audit HIGH 1). Implementing this needs an `ExprPlan`-level marker propagated to the function dispatch (separate from the existing `is_aggregate_function` / RangeAwareFn machinery). | `crates/ql-functions/src/scalar_fns.rs::count` matches only `Value::Number`; pinned by `count_direct_bool_and_numeric_text_v1_divergence` | Engine | Engine Phase 4.10 (function library wave 2) or earlier if real-world workbooks surface it |

    75	| GAP-F-09 | `NETWORKDAYS` holidays-array arg not supported in V1. Excel canon: 3rd arg is an optional range of holiday dates that are subtracted from the working-day count.     77	| GAP-F-11 | `YEARFRAC` basis 0 (US NASD 30/360) uses the European 30/360 algorithm (basis 4) in V1.     79	| GAP-T-01 | Time-zone offset acquisition for `NOW()` / `TODAY()` local-time policy not implemented.     80	| GAP-F-12 | `WorkbookRuntime::format_cache` can stale if `Workbook::formats_mut()` mutates the FormatTable AFTER `read_display` has cached the General fallback for a (then-unknown) id. Direct low-level mutation through `formats_mut()` (loader / tests only) on a live runtime is the pathological sequence.     82	| ~~GAP-F-05~~ | ~~`ScalarFn` cannot carry range-vs-scalar argument metadata.~~ **CLOSED in Engine Phase 4.3 V2 (W5-53; refined W5-54).** Approach (a) — parallel `RangeAwareFn = fn(&[FnArg]) -> Value` table — chosen instead of full migration of the ~60 existing scalar entries. `FnArg` lives in `crates/ql-functions/src/range_aware_fns.rs`. **Final shape (W5-54 refactor):** `enum FnArg { Scalar(Value), Range { values: Vec<Value>, rows: usize, cols: usize } }` — `Range` is a struct variant carrying 2D shape, invariant `rows * cols == values.len()`. 1D consumers (SUMIF, COUNTIF) pattern-match `{ values, .. }` and ignore shape; 2D consumers (VLOOKUP, HLOOKUP, INDEX, MATCH) consume `rows`/`cols`. `FunctionRegistry` carries a second table `range_aware_fns: HashMap<&'static str, RangeAwareFn>` with `register_range_aware` / `lookup_range_aware`. Cross-table name collision panics at registration. Eval dispatch in `crates/ql-exec/src/scalar.rs::eval_scalar_with_cache` checks `lookup_range_aware` FIRST and constructs `Vec<FnArg>` from `ExprPlan` args (AggregateNameRef → `Range { values, rows, cols }` via `env.read_range_with_shape`, other plans → `Scalar` via recursive eval). SUMIF + COUNTIF shipped on this infra W5-53; SUMIFS / AVERAGEIF / SUMPRODUCT / VLOOKUP / HLOOKUP / MATCH / INDEX / CHOOSE + LARGE/SMALL/RANK/MEDIAN/MODE all live on it by W5-58. **W5-60 closure** added strict 2D shape validation in IFS family + SUMPRODUCT (`(rows, cols)` consensus, not flat length); `env.read_range_with_shape` distinguishes open-ended (clamp) from bounded (preserve+pad-with-Blank) ranges. 5 e2e tests in `workbook_runtime` + range-fn unit tests + 4 registry conflict/disjoint tests + extended `is_aggregate_function_lists_only_registered_aggregates` invariant test + W5-60 6 new 2D-shape regression tests. |

   156	- **GAP-S-01** (single overlay per chunk, user + formula outputs conflated) — closed in Engine Phase 3.5: parallel `user_overlays` + `computed_overlays` with read cascade user → computed → base.
   157	- **GAP-B-01** (named-range aggregate eval returned `#CALC!`) — fully closed in Engine Phase 3.6 (W5-39): the scalar evaluator now computes the real aggregate for SUM/AVERAGE/MIN/MAX/COUNT/PRODUCT over a single named-range arg + caches the result on `CalcgraphSession`.
   158	- **GAP-R-04** (volatile function invalidation) — closed in Engine Phase 3.7 (W5-40): `mark_volatile_dirty` fans out volatile cells + downstream chain; NOW/TODAY/RAND/RANDBETWEEN registered; deterministic test RNG via `set_test_rng_seed`.
   159	- **GAP-R-05** (value-equality short-circuit) — closed in Engine Phase 3.8 (W5-41): `recompute_dirty` skips formulas whose direct cell deps stayed at their prior values. `RecomputeResult.skipped_value_equality` exposes the count.
   160	- **GAP-D-03** (no `docs/architecture/` directory) — closed in Engine Phase 3.1 + 3.10 (W5-34, W5-43): `docs/architecture/calcgraph-runtime.md` exists with full Phase 3.x rewrite.
   161	- **GAP-D-04** (Excel compatibility matrix file absent) — closed in Engine Phase 4.2 (W5-45): `docs/compat/excel-matrix.md` ships with 217 rows + `scripts/report-compat-coverage.sh` CI reporter.
   162	- **Phase 3 megaudit H2** (`on_set_name` not transitive) — closed in 3.10 audit-closure commit (W5-43): BFS-fanout fix + regression test `h2_set_name_propagates_dirty_transitively`.
   163	- **Phase 2B.7 audit-closure fixes** (correctness): orphan op-log entries on `add_sheet`(chunk_rows=0 / SheetId::MAX), `set_name` mutate-first divergence, `set_value` / `clear_formula` partial-pair non-atomicity, `transaction::commit` post-mutation log append. All fixed. `Workbook` and `OpLog` proven `Send + Sync` at compile time. See `docs/audits/2026-05-12-phase-2B.md`.
   164	- **GAP-G-01** (rebind staleness — Phase 3.10 megaudit H3/H4) — closed in Engine Phase 4 pre-V2: rebind path in W5-50 (`extract_and_register_deps`), clear-formula path in W5-52 (`on_clear_formula`; W5-52 audit closure caught the clear-path miss). `Graph::clear_outgoing` + `Graph::clear_range_deps_for_formula` + `StripeIndex::clear_for_formula` (with reverse `formula_to_stripe_keys`). Architectural decision: `docs/architecture/2026-05-13-graph-storage-decision.md`.
   165	- **GAP-G-03** (range deps not scheduler edges — Phase 3.10 megaudit H1) — closed in Engine Phase 4 pre-V2 (W5-50): `topo::schedule_with_supplemental` accepts caller-injected temp adjacency; `CalcgraphSession::build_range_supplemental` constructs F→G edges for dirty formula pairs where G is inside one of F's ranges. Architectural decision: `docs/architecture/2026-05-13-graph-storage-decision.md`.
   166	- **GAP-F-05** (`ScalarFn` cannot carry range-vs-scalar metadata; filed in W5-52 mega-audit) — closed in Engine Phase 4.3 V2 (W5-53; refined W5-54 + W5-60): parallel `RangeAwareFn = fn(&[FnArg]) -> Value` table with `enum FnArg { Scalar(Value), Range { values: Vec<Value>, rows: usize, cols: usize } }` (struct variant since W5-54 — carries 2D shape, invariant `rows * cols == values.len()`). SUMIF + COUNTIF shipped W5-53; lookup family + IFS + stats followed through W5-58. W5-60 closure added strict 2D-shape validation for IFS family + SUMPRODUCT.

   351	/// Phase 2A.8: workbook-scope defined names. The on-disk wire format mirrors
   352	/// `ql_storage::NameTable` + `NamedTarget`, projected into serializable shapes.
   353	///
   354	/// Names are stored in canonical (upper-case) form, matching `NameTable::set`'s
   355	/// canonicalization. Order is sorted ascending by name for deterministic diffs.
   356	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   357	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   358	pub struct NamesSection {
   359	    pub entries: Vec<NamedEntry>,
   360	}
   361	
   362	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   363	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   364	pub struct NamedEntry {
   365	    pub name: String,
   366	    pub target: NamedTargetWire,
   367	    /// **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D):** name scope.
   368	    /// - `None` (default; v1-v4 wire) = workbook-scoped.
   369	    /// - `Some(sheet_id)` = sheet-scoped; sheet-scoped beats workbook-
   370	    ///   scoped at lookup time (Excel canon, XS-4-03).
   371	    ///
   372	    /// `serde(default)` keeps the field optional in v1-v4 payloads;
   373	    /// `skip_serializing_if` keeps v1-v4 round-trips byte-stable for
   374	    /// workbook-scoped names (the common case).
   375	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   376	    pub scope: Option<u16>,
   377	}
   378	
   379	/// Wire-format mirror of `ql_storage::NamedTarget`. Each variant is tagged
   380	/// explicitly so the on-disk shape is self-describing and survives schema bumps.
   381	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   382	#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "lowercase")]
   383	pub enum NamedTargetWire {
   384	    /// `NamedTarget::Cell` — single-cell anchor at `$Sheet!$Row,$Col`.
   385	    Cell { sheet: u16, row: u32, col: u32 },
   386	    /// `NamedTarget::Range` — rectangular range.
   387	    Range {
   388	        sheet: u16,
   389	        start_row: u32,
   390	        start_col: u32,
   391	        end_row: u32,
   392	        end_col: u32,
   393	    },
   394	    /// `NamedTarget::Constant(Value)` — reuses the cell wire-value vocabulary.
   395	    Constant { value: CellWireValue },
   396	    /// `NamedTarget::Formula(Arc<str>)` — raw formula source (without `=`).
   397	    Formula { source: String },
   398	}
   399	
   400	/// JSONL cell record — one line per non-blank or formula-bearing cell.
   401	///
   402	/// Tagged enum representation keeps the wire format compact + self-describing:
   403	/// `{"row":5,"col":3,"value":{"Number":42.0}}`.
   404	///
   405	/// - **W5-9** added the optional `formula` field. If `Some`, the cell carries a
   406	///   formula whose evaluated result is `value`. If `None` (or absent in the
   407	///   JSON), the cell is a literal value.
   408	/// - **Phase 2A.8** added `deny_unknown_fields` per audit M9. Any unrecognized
   409	///   JSON field is rejected loudly at parse time.
   410	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   411	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   412	pub struct CellRecord {
   413	    pub row: u32,
   414	    pub col: u32,
   415	    pub value: CellWireValue,
   416	    /// Formula source (without leading `=`). Phase 1 W5-9. Omitted from JSON when None.
   417	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   418	    pub formula: Option<String>,
   419	}
   420	
   421	/// Wire-format mirror of `ql_types::Value`. Phase 1 W5-6 doesn't add Serialize to
   422	/// ql-types directly (keeps the types crate lean); ql-io owns the encoding.
   423	///
   424	/// Phase 2A.8 (megaudit M11): added `Pending` variant for formula-bearing cells
   425	/// whose saved value would be `Value::Blank` (because the formula hasn't been
   426	/// evaluated yet, or its result really is Blank). Previously this case was
   427	/// encoded as `Error("#NULL!")`, conflating "not yet evaluated" with a real
   428	/// Excel `#NULL!` error. The v2 reader recognizes `Pending` as "needs
   429	/// recompute"; for v1 files, the loader auto-rewrites `Error(#NULL!)` to
   430	/// `Pending` when the same cell has a `formula` field.
   431	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   432	pub enum CellWireValue {
   433	    Number(f64),
   434	    Boolean(bool),
   435	    Text(String),
   436	    Error(String), // Stored as the canonical "#REF!" / "#VALUE!" / etc. text form.
   437	    /// Formula-bearing cell with no evaluated value. Recompute resolves it.
   438	    Pending,
   439	}
   440	
   441	impl CellWireValue {
   442	    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<Self> {
   443	        match v {
   444	            Value::Blank => None,
   445	            Value::Number(n) => Some(CellWireValue::Number(*n)),
   446	            Value::Boolean(b) => Some(CellWireValue::Boolean(*b)),
   447	            Value::Text(s) => Some(CellWireValue::Text(s.as_ref().to_owned())),
   448	            Value::Error(e) => Some(CellWireValue::Error(error_to_canonical_text(*e))),
   449	        }
   450	    }
   451	
   452	    /// Decode the wire value into a `ql_types::Value`. `Pending` decodes to
   453	    /// `Value::Blank` — the cell is in a transient state until recompute runs.
   454	    pub fn to_value(&self) -> Result<Value, QbookError> {
   455	        match self {
   456	            CellWireValue::Number(n) => Ok(Value::number(*n)),
   457	            CellWireValue::Boolean(b) => Ok(Value::Boolean(*b)),
   458	            CellWireValue::Text(s) => Ok(Value::text(s.as_str())),
   459	            CellWireValue::Error(s) => match parse_canonical_error_text(s) {
   460	                Some(e) => Ok(Value::Error(e)),
   461	                None => Err(QbookError::MalformedCell {
   462	                    file: PathBuf::new(),
   463	                    line: 0,
   464	                    detail: format!("unknown error variant: {s:?}"),
   465	                }),
   466	            },
   467	            CellWireValue::Pending => Ok(Value::Blank),
   468	        }
   469	    }
   470	
   471	    /// True iff this is the Phase 2A.8 Pending sentinel (formula-bearing cell
   472	    /// with no evaluated value).
   473	    pub fn is_pending(&self) -> bool {
   474	        matches!(self, CellWireValue::Pending)
   475	    }
   476	}
   477	
   478	impl NamedTargetWire {
   479	    /// Project a runtime `NamedTarget` into the wire shape for serialization.
   480	    pub fn from_target(t: &NamedTarget) -> Self {
   481	        match t {
   482	            NamedTarget::Cell(addr) => NamedTargetWire::Cell {
   483	                sheet: addr.sheet,
   484	                row: addr.row,
   485	                col: addr.col,
   486	            },
   487	            NamedTarget::Range(r) => NamedTargetWire::Range {
   488	                sheet: r.sheet,
   489	                start_row: r.start_row,
   490	                start_col: r.start_col,
   491	                end_row: r.end_row,
   492	                end_col: r.end_col,
   493	            },
   494	            NamedTarget::Constant(v) => NamedTargetWire::Constant {
   495	                // NamedTarget::Constant(Value::Blank) is unusual but valid; map
   496	                // to Number(0.0) sentinel? No — preserve the variant by emitting
   497	                // Error("BLANK_SENTINEL")? Also wrong. The cleanest answer: refuse
   498	                // to serialize Blank constants — the user shouldn't have one,
   499	                // and we'd round-trip-lose it anyway.
   500	                //
   501	                // In practice, `from_value(&Value::Blank)` returns None. So to
   502	                // serialize a Blank constant we'd need a dedicated wire variant.
   503	                // For Phase 2A.8 we deny it at save time via the conversion
   504	                // helper below; this match arm assumes from_value returns Some.
   505	                value: CellWireValue::from_value(v).unwrap_or(CellWireValue::Pending),
   506	            },
   507	            NamedTarget::Formula(src) => NamedTargetWire::Formula {
   508	                source: src.as_ref().to_owned(),
   509	            },
   510	        }
   511	    }
   512	
   513	    /// Decode the wire shape back into a `NamedTarget`. Carries the name only
   514	    /// for error reporting; the caller embeds the result in a `NameTable`.
   515	    pub fn to_target(&self, name_for_error: &str) -> Result<NamedTarget, QbookError> {
   516	        match self {
   517	            NamedTargetWire::Cell { sheet, row, col } => {
   518	                if *row > MAX_ROW {
   519	                    return Err(QbookError::MalformedName {
   520	                        name: name_for_error.to_owned(),
   521	                        reason: format!("Cell.row {row} exceeds MAX_ROW {MAX_ROW}"),
   522	                    });
   523	                }
   524	                if *col > MAX_COLUMN {
   525	                    return Err(QbookError::MalformedName {
   526	                        name: name_for_error.to_owned(),
   527	                        reason: format!("Cell.col {col} exceeds MAX_COLUMN {MAX_COLUMN}"),
   528	                    });
   529	                }
   530	                Ok(NamedTarget::Cell(Address::new(*sheet, *row, *col)))
   531	            }
   532	            NamedTargetWire::Range {
   533	                sheet,
   534	                start_row,
   535	                start_col,
   536	                end_row,
   537	                end_col,
   538	            } => {
   539	                for (label, v, max) in [
   540	                    ("start_row", *start_row, MAX_ROW),
   541	                    ("start_col", *start_col, MAX_COLUMN),
   542	                    ("end_row", *end_row, MAX_ROW),
   543	                    ("end_col", *end_col, MAX_COLUMN),
   544	                ] {
   545	                    if v > max {
   546	                        return Err(QbookError::MalformedName {
   547	                            name: name_for_error.to_owned(),
   548	                            reason: format!("Range.{label} {v} exceeds max {max}"),
   549	                        });
   550	                    }
   551	                }
   552	                Ok(NamedTarget::Range(Range::new(
   553	                    *sheet, *start_row, *start_col, *end_row, *end_col,
   554	                )))
   555	            }
   556	            NamedTargetWire::Constant { value } => {
   557	                let v = value.to_value().map_err(|e| match e {
   558	                    QbookError::MalformedCell { detail, .. } => QbookError::MalformedName {
   559	                        name: name_for_error.to_owned(),
   560	                        reason: format!("Constant decode: {detail}"),
   561	                    },
   562	                    other => other,
   563	                })?;
   564	                Ok(NamedTarget::Constant(v))
   565	            }
   566	            NamedTargetWire::Formula { source } => {
   567	                Ok(NamedTarget::Formula(std::sync::Arc::from(source.as_str())))
   568	            }
   569	        }
   570	    }
   571	}
   572	
   573	/// Map `ErrorValue` to its canonical Excel-style text form (`#REF!`, `#VALUE!`, etc.).
   574	/// Used by both wire-format serialization and the user-visible representation per spec.
   575	pub fn error_to_canonical_text(e: ErrorValue) -> String {
   576	    match e {
   577	        ErrorValue::Ref => "#REF!".to_string(),
   578	        ErrorValue::Value => "#VALUE!".to_string(),
   579	        ErrorValue::NA => "#N/A".to_string(),
   580	        ErrorValue::DivZero => "#DIV/0!".to_string(),
   581	        ErrorValue::Null => "#NULL!".to_string(),
   582	        ErrorValue::Num => "#NUM!".to_string(),
   583	        ErrorValue::Name => "#NAME?".to_string(),
   584	        ErrorValue::Spill => "#SPILL!".to_string(),
   585	        ErrorValue::Calc => "#CALC!".to_string(),
   586	        ErrorValue::Disconnected => "#DISCONNECTED!".to_string(),
   587	        ErrorValue::Binding => "#BINDING!".to_string(),
   588	        ErrorValue::Timeout => "#TIMEOUT!".to_string(),
   589	        ErrorValue::Permission => "#PERMISSION!".to_string(),
   590	        ErrorValue::AINotAvailable => "#AI_NOT_AVAILABLE_V1".to_string(),
   591	        ErrorValue::Circ => "#CIRC!".to_string(),
   592	    }
   593	}
   594	
   595	fn parse_canonical_error_text(s: &str) -> Option<ErrorValue> {
   596	    Some(match s {
   597	        "#REF!" => ErrorValue::Ref,
   598	        "#VALUE!" => ErrorValue::Value,
   599	        "#N/A" => ErrorValue::NA,
   600	        "#DIV/0!" => ErrorValue::DivZero,
   601	        "#NULL!" => ErrorValue::Null,
   602	        "#NUM!" => ErrorValue::Num,
   603	        "#NAME?" => ErrorValue::Name,
   604	        "#SPILL!" => ErrorValue::Spill,
   605	        "#CALC!" => ErrorValue::Calc,
   606	        "#DISCONNECTED!" => ErrorValue::Disconnected,
   607	        "#BINDING!" => ErrorValue::Binding,
   608	        "#TIMEOUT!" => ErrorValue::Timeout,
   609	        "#PERMISSION!" => ErrorValue::Permission,
   610	        "#AI_NOT_AVAILABLE_V1" => ErrorValue::AINotAvailable,
   611	        "#CIRC!" => ErrorValue::Circ,
   612	        _ => return None,
   613	    })
   614	}
   615	
   616	/// Save a `Workbook` to `path` (a `.qbook/` directory). **Crash-safe atomic save**
   617	/// per the Phase 2A.8 megaudit H2 closure:
   618	///
   619	/// 1. Write the new workbook to `<path>.tmp-save-<random>`.
   620	/// 2. If `path` already exists, rename it to `<path>.bak-<random>`.
   621	/// 3. Rename the temp directory into `path`.
   622	/// 4. Best-effort remove the backup; on failure, log a warning to stderr but
   623	///    DO NOT silently swallow (megaudit M8). The next `load_workbook` will
   624	///    detect the orphan via `recover_from_crashed_save` and clean it up.
   625	///
   626	/// **Invariant**: between any two adjacent steps, at least one of `path` or
   627	/// `<path>.bak-<random>` contains a complete valid workbook. A crash at any
   628	/// instant leaves a recoverable on-disk state.
   629	///
   630	/// Cross-filesystem note: the temp and backup paths are *siblings* of the
   631	/// target, sharing its parent directory. POSIX `rename(2)` is atomic within a
   632	/// single filesystem; the sibling-only design preserves that property.
   633	pub fn save_workbook(wb: &Workbook, name: &str, path: &Path) -> Result<(), QbookError> {
   634	    save_workbook_extending(wb, name, path, |_| Ok(()))
   635	}
   636	
   637	/// Phase 2A.3.c (2026-05-12): closure-based extension primitive. Same atomic-save
   638	/// protocol as `save_workbook`, with an additional hook (`extend`) that fires
   639	/// AFTER `write_workbook_to_dir` populates the temp directory but BEFORE the
   640	/// atomic `rename(temp → target)`. Used by `ql_oplog::save_workbook_with_oplog`
   641	/// to write `oplog.bin` as a sidecar — it rides the same atomic rename and
   642	/// recovers via the same crash-rollback path.
   643	///
   644	/// The closure receives the temp directory path. It can `fs::write` arbitrary
   645	/// files into it. The atomic-save invariant still holds: at every step
   646	/// boundary, at least one of {target, `<target>.bak-<random>`} contains a
   647	/// complete valid workbook + any sidecars the closure wrote.
   648	///
   649	/// Closure failure cleans up the temp directory and propagates the error —
   650	/// same semantics as a `write_workbook_to_dir` failure.
   651	///
   652	/// Public `save_workbook` is a thin wrapper that calls this with a no-op
   653	/// closure; callers without sidecars should keep using `save_workbook`.
   654	pub fn save_workbook_extending<F>(
   655	    wb: &Workbook,
   656	    name: &str,
   657	    path: &Path,
   658	    extend: F,
   659	) -> Result<(), QbookError>
   660	where
   661	    F: FnOnce(&Path) -> Result<(), QbookError>,
   662	{
   663	    let paths = make_save_paths(path)?;
   664	
   665	    // Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 H5: refuse to save into a path that exists
   666	    // but is NOT a directory. `path.exists() && !path.is_dir()` means a
   667	    // regular file (or symlink, FIFO, etc.) is sitting where the workbook
   668	    // directory should be. The previous code would have happily renamed
   669	    // that file to `<base>.bak-<suffix>`, then installed our directory in
   670	    // its place — the user's original file becomes an orphan no one will
   671	    // ever find (recovery would try `remove_dir_all` on it and fail with
   672	    // `NotADirectory`). Loud refusal is the right answer.
   673	    if path.exists() && !path.is_dir() {
   674	        return Err(QbookError::InvalidPath {
   675	            path: path.to_path_buf(),
   676	            reason: "target exists but is not a directory",
   677	        });
   678	    }
   679	
   680	    // Step 1: write everything to the temp dir. If this fails, the original
   681	    // target (if any) is untouched and we clean up the partial temp.
   682	    if let Err(e) = write_workbook_to_dir(wb, name, &paths.temp) {
   683	        if let Err(cleanup_err) = fs::remove_dir_all(&paths.temp) {
   684	            // The original save error is what the caller wants; the cleanup
   685	            // failure goes to stderr per the M8 audit (don't silence).
   686	            eprintln!(
   687	                "warning: failed to clean partial temp directory {:?} after save error: {cleanup_err}",
   688	                paths.temp
   689	            );
   690	        }
   691	        return Err(e);
   692	    }
   693	
   694	    // Phase 2A.3.c: extension hook fires after the engine has finished
   695	    // populating the temp dir (envelope + sheets + marker), but before the
   696	    // atomic rename. Sidecars written here become part of the all-or-nothing
   697	    // visibility guarantee.
   698	    if let Err(e) = extend(&paths.temp) {
   699	        if let Err(cleanup_err) = fs::remove_dir_all(&paths.temp) {
   700	            eprintln!(
   701	                "warning: failed to clean partial temp directory {:?} after extend error: {cleanup_err}",
   702	                paths.temp
   703	            );
   704	        }
   705	        return Err(e);
   706	    }
   707	
   708	    // Step 2: if target exists, move it aside (NOT remove). After this, the
   709	    // old state is at `.bak-<random>` and the target is missing.
   710	    let had_existing = path.exists();
   711	    if had_existing {
   712	        fs::rename(path, &paths.backup)?;
   713	    }
   714	
   715	    // Step 3: install the new state at the target. If this fails after step 2,
   716	    // we have `.bak-<random>` only and no target — recovery on next load will
   717	    // roll back. To avoid leaving the temp dir orphaned in that case, attempt
   718	    // a rollback ourselves before returning.
   719	    if let Err(install_err) = fs::rename(&paths.temp, path) {
   720	        // Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 M5: when install fails AND restore also
   721	        // fails, surface a compound error (was: silently `eprintln!`ed the
   722	        // restore failure and returned only the install error).
   723	        let restore_err: Option<std::io::Error> = if had_existing {
   724	            fs::rename(&paths.backup, path).err()
   725	        } else {
   726	            None
   727	        };
   728	        // The temp dir didn't move into place; try to clean it up.
   729	        if let Err(cleanup_err) = fs::remove_dir_all(&paths.temp) {
   730	            eprintln!(
   731	                "warning: failed to clean temp directory {:?} after rename failure: {cleanup_err}",
   732	                paths.temp
   733	            );
   734	        }
   735	        if let Some(restore_err) = restore_err {
   736	            // Both failed → compound user-visible error.
   737	            return Err(QbookError::AtomicSaveRollbackFailed {
   738	                backup_path: paths.backup.clone(),
   739	                install: install_err.to_string(),
   740	                restore: restore_err.to_string(),
   741	            });
   742	        }
   743	        // Restore succeeded (or no prior target existed): the user's prior
   744	        // workbook is intact at `path`. Return the install error so the
   745	        // caller knows the save itself failed.
   746	        return Err(QbookError::Io(install_err));
   747	    }
   748	
   749	    // Step 4: best-effort backup cleanup. On failure, log loudly — but the
   750	    // save itself succeeded, so we return Ok. `recover_from_crashed_save` on
   751	    // a future load will eventually clean up the orphan.
   752	    if had_existing {
   753	        if let Err(e) = fs::remove_dir_all(&paths.backup) {
   754	            eprintln!(
   755	                "warning: save succeeded but backup cleanup failed at {:?}: {e}",
   756	                paths.backup
   757	            );
   758	        }
   759	    }
   760	    Ok(())

   762	
   763	/// Phase 2A.8 audit M10: each save uses a fresh, collision-resistant suffix so
   764	/// concurrent saves from two threads in the same process never share temp /
   765	/// backup paths. The suffix mixes wall-clock nanos, PID, and the calling
   766	/// thread's id — sufficient uniqueness for the IDE save-path workload without
   767	/// pulling in a random-source crate.
   768	fn save_session_suffix() -> String {
   769	    let nanos = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   770	        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   771	        .map(|d| d.as_nanos() as u64)
   772	        .unwrap_or(0);
   773	    let pid = std::process::id() as u64;
   774	    // ThreadId formats as "ThreadId(N)" — hash via length + a fixed mixer to
   775	    // get a u64 contribution. Stable within a process.
   776	    let tid_str = format!("{:?}", std::thread::current().id());
   777	    let tid_hash = tid_str.bytes().fold(0u64, |acc, b| {
   778	        acc.wrapping_mul(0x100000001B3).wrapping_add(b as u64)
   779	    });
   780	    let mixed = nanos
   781	        .wrapping_mul(0x9E3779B97F4A7C15)
   782	        .wrapping_add(pid.wrapping_mul(0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9))
   783	        ^ tid_hash;
   784	    format!("{mixed:016x}")
   785	}
   786	
   787	/// Resolved temp + backup paths for one save invocation. Sharing the suffix
   788	/// between temp and backup lets `recover_from_crashed_save` link them
   789	/// unambiguously if a crash occurs mid-protocol.
   790	struct SavePaths {
   791	    temp: PathBuf,
   792	    backup: PathBuf,
   793	}
   794	
   795	/// Derive temp + backup sibling paths for `target`. Phase 2A.8 audit M9
   796	/// closure: degenerate paths (no parent, no file_name) error loudly via
   797	/// `QbookError::InvalidPath` rather than the prior `unwrap_or` silent fallback.
   798	fn make_save_paths(target: &Path) -> Result<SavePaths, QbookError> {
   799	    let parent = target.parent().ok_or_else(|| QbookError::InvalidPath {
   800	        path: target.to_path_buf(),
   801	        reason: "no parent directory",
   802	    })?;
   803	    let basename = target
   804	        .file_name()
   805	        .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
   806	        .ok_or_else(|| QbookError::InvalidPath {
   807	            path: target.to_path_buf(),
   808	            reason: "no file name",
   809	        })?;
   810	    // Refuse to save into a target whose basename is itself a temp/backup name
   811	    // — that would let a path like `foo.bak-abc/` collide with our own
   812	    // backup-rename protocol. Phase 2A.8 conservative gate; the IDE shouldn't
   813	    // pick names like this, but a hostile caller could.
   814	    if basename.contains(".tmp-save-") || basename.contains(".bak-") {
   815	        return Err(QbookError::InvalidPath {
   816	            path: target.to_path_buf(),
   817	            reason: "basename collides with atomic-save protocol naming",
   818	        });
   819	    }
   820	    let suffix = save_session_suffix();
   821	    Ok(SavePaths {
   822	        temp: parent.join(format!("{basename}.tmp-save-{suffix}")),
   823	        backup: parent.join(format!("{basename}.bak-{suffix}")),
   824	    })
   825	}
   826	
   827	/// Phase 2A.8 audit H2 recovery protocol. Called at the top of
   828	/// `load_workbook`. Detects orphan `.bak-<random>` siblings of `target` and
   829	/// either rolls forward (target is intact, just clean up bak) or rolls back
   830	/// (target missing/invalid, rename bak → target).
   831	///
   832	/// If multiple `.bak-*` orphans exist (unlikely; would require multiple
   833	/// crashes across save sessions), we conservatively refuse to load and ask
   834	/// the caller to manually resolve. Returning an error here is safer than
   835	/// guessing which backup is "the right one."
   836	fn recover_from_crashed_save(target: &Path) -> Result<(), QbookError> {
   837	    let Some(parent) = target.parent() else {
   838	        return Ok(()); // No parent, nothing to scan.
   839	    };
   840	    let basename = match target.file_name() {
   841	        Some(s) => s.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
   842	        None => return Ok(()),
   843	    };
   844	    let bak_prefix = format!("{basename}.bak-");
   845	
   846	    // Scan the parent dir for orphan .bak-* siblings.
   847	    // Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 H1: ONLY consider directories that carry our
   848	    // atomic-save marker file (`is_engine_owned_backup`). A user-created
   849	    // sibling like `book.qbook.bak-2025-review` shares the filename prefix
   850	    // but lacks the marker — recovery leaves it alone.
   851	    let mut orphans: Vec<PathBuf> = Vec::new();
   852	    let read_dir = match fs::read_dir(parent) {
   853	        Ok(it) => it,
   854	        Err(_) => return Ok(()), // Parent unreadable; load will fail downstream with a clearer error.
   855	    };
   856	    for entry in read_dir.flatten() {
   857	        if let Some(name) = entry.file_name().to_str() {
   858	            if name.starts_with(&bak_prefix) {
   859	                let p = entry.path();
   860	                if is_engine_owned_backup(&p) {
   861	                    orphans.push(p);
   862	                }
   863	                // Else: user-created sibling that just happens to match the
   864	                // `.bak-*` prefix. Not ours, don't touch.
   865	            }
   866	        }
   867	    }
   868	
   869	    if orphans.is_empty() {
   870	        return Ok(());
   871	    }
   872	
   873	    // Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 M6: when the target is valid AND we have
   874	    // multiple orphans, they're ALL stale (a valid target can only result
   875	    // from a single most-recent successful save; any older `.bak-*` is from
   876	    // a prior save whose step-4 cleanup never completed). Clean them all up
   877	    // instead of refusing to load — accumulated `eprintln!` warnings from
   878	    // repeated step-4 failures would otherwise create a permanent deadlock.
   879	    if target_envelope_is_valid(target) {
   880	        for bak in &orphans {
   881	            if let Err(e) = fs::remove_dir_all(bak) {
   882	                eprintln!(
   883	                    "warning: load detected orphan backup {bak:?} but cleanup failed: {e}; \
   884	                     target is valid, so load proceeds"
   885	                );
   886	            }
   887	        }
   888	        return Ok(());
   889	    }
   890	
   891	    // Target is missing or invalid. We need to restore from a backup.
   892	    // Multi-orphan case here IS ambiguous (we don't know which backup
   893	    // matches the missing target); refuse and ask the user.
   894	    if orphans.len() > 1 {
   895	        return Err(QbookError::InvalidPath {
   896	            path: target.to_path_buf(),
   897	            reason: "target is missing/invalid AND multiple .bak-* siblings present; \
   898	                     ambiguous crash recovery — resolve manually before loading",
   899	        });
   900	    }
   901	    let bak = orphans.into_iter().next().expect("len == 1");
   902	
   903	    // Step 2 completed but step 3 didn't (or step 3 partially completed
   904	    // and left an invalid target). Roll back: restore the old state.
   905	    if target.exists() {
   906	        if let Err(e) = fs::remove_dir_all(target) {
   907	            return Err(QbookError::Io(e));
   908	        }
   909	    }
   910	    // Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 M4: concurrent loader race. Two loaders
   911	    // seeing the same orphan can race here — one wins, the other sees
   912	    // `NotFound` because the bak is already consumed. If we lose the
   913	    // rename race, re-check whether the target is NOW valid (the other
   914	    // loader's rename succeeded and installed it). Only return the I/O
   915	    // error if the rollback genuinely failed.
   916	    if let Err(rename_err) = fs::rename(&bak, target) {
   917	        if target_envelope_is_valid(target) {
   918	            // Another loader won the race and the rollback is already done.
   919	            // We're good — proceed with load.
   920	            return Ok(());
   921	        }
   922	        return Err(QbookError::Io(rename_err));
   923	    }
   924	    Ok(())
   925	}
   926	
   927	/// Cheap envelope-validity check used by recovery. The full load is the real
   928	/// validity test, but recovery needs a quick yes/no to decide forward vs
   929	/// back. We check: directory exists, `workbook.toml` parses, every claimed
   930	/// sheet JSONL file exists. We DO NOT parse JSONL line-by-line — that's the
   931	/// load path's job.
   932	fn target_envelope_is_valid(target: &Path) -> bool {
   933	    if !target.is_dir() {
   934	        return false;
   935	    }
   936	    let toml_path = target.join("workbook.toml");
   937	    if !toml_path.is_file() {
   938	        return false;
   939	    }
   940	    let toml_str = match fs::read_to_string(&toml_path) {
   941	        Ok(s) => s,
   942	        Err(_) => return false,
   943	    };
   944	    let envelope: WorkbookEnvelope = match toml::from_str(&toml_str) {
   945	        Ok(e) => e,
   946	        Err(_) => return false,
   947	    };
   948	    let sheets_dir = target.join("sheets");
   949	    for sheet_env in &envelope.sheets {
   950	        let sheet_path = sheets_dir.join(format!("{}.jsonl", sheet_env.id));
   951	        if !sheet_path.is_file() {
   952	            return false;
   953	        }
   954	    }
   955	    true
   956	}
   957	
   958	/// Inner write that populates `dir` with `workbook.toml` + `sheets/*.jsonl`. Used by
   959	/// `save_workbook` against a temp dir; not a public surface (the atomic-save wrapper
   960	/// is what callers want).
   961	fn write_workbook_to_dir(wb: &Workbook, name: &str, dir: &Path) -> Result<(), QbookError> {
   962	    fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;
   963	    let sheets_dir = dir.join("sheets");
   964	    fs::create_dir_all(&sheets_dir)?;
   965	
   966	    // Build the envelope.
   967	    let mut sheet_envelopes: Vec<SheetEnvelope> = Vec::new();
   968	    for sheet_id in 0..(wb.sheet_count() as SheetId) {
   969	        let sheet = wb.sheet(sheet_id).expect("sheet_count was wrong");
   970	        let bounds = sheet.bounds();
   971	        // **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** serialize the per-sheet cell-format
   972	        // overlay if it has any entries. Sorted by (row, col) for diffability.
   973	        let format_overlay = {
   974	            let overlay = sheet.format_overlay();
   975	            if overlay.is_empty() {
   976	                None
   977	            } else {
   978	                let mut entries: Vec<FormatOverlayEntry> = overlay
   979	                    .iter()
   980	                    .map(|((r, c), fid)| FormatOverlayEntry {
   981	                        row: r,
   982	                        col: c,
   983	                        id: fid.0,
   984	                    })
   985	                    .collect();
   986	                entries.sort_by_key(|e| (e.row, e.col));
   987	                Some(entries)
   988	            }
   989	        };
   990	        sheet_envelopes.push(SheetEnvelope {
   991	            id: sheet_id,
   992	            name: sheet.name().to_owned(),
   993	            chunk_rows: sheet_chunk_rows(sheet),
   994	            row_extent: bounds.row_extent,
   995	            col_extent: bounds.col_extent,
   996	            format_overlay,
   997	        });
   998	    }
   999	
  1000	    // Phase 2A.8: serialize the workbook's NameTable. Sorted ascending by
  1001	    // (scope, name) for deterministic on-disk diffs (workbook-scoped first
  1002	    // since `None < Some` under derive(Ord); then by name). Omitted (None)
  1003	    // when both workbook-scoped table AND every sheet's scoped_names table
  1004	    // are empty, so v1-reader compatibility round-trips cleanly for the
  1005	    // empty-names common case.
  1006	    //
  1007	    // **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D):** collects workbook-scoped names first, then
  1008	    // sheet-scoped names from each sheet's `scoped_names` table. The
  1009	    // `scope` field on `NamedEntry` distinguishes the two; legacy v1-v4
  1010	    // readers emit only the workbook-scoped entries (no per-sheet names
  1011	    // existed pre-v5).
  1012	    let names_section = {
  1013	        let mut entries: Vec<NamedEntry> = Vec::new();
  1014	        for (name, target) in wb.names().iter() {
  1015	            entries.push(NamedEntry {
  1016	                name: name.as_ref().to_owned(),
  1017	                target: NamedTargetWire::from_target(target),
  1018	                scope: None,
  1019	            });
  1020	        }
  1021	        for sheet_id in 0..wb.sheet_count() as ql_types::SheetId {
  1022	            if let Some(sheet) = wb.sheet(sheet_id) {
  1023	                for (name, target) in sheet.scoped_names().iter() {
  1024	                    entries.push(NamedEntry {
  1025	                        name: name.as_ref().to_owned(),
  1026	                        target: NamedTargetWire::from_target(target),
  1027	                        scope: Some(sheet_id),
  1028	                    });
  1029	                }
  1030	            }
  1031	        }
  1032	        if entries.is_empty() {
  1033	            None
  1034	        } else {
  1035	            // Deterministic ordering: workbook-scoped (scope=None) entries
  1036	            // first, then sheet-scoped grouped by sheet id; within each
  1037	            // group, names sorted ascending.
  1038	            entries.sort_by(|a, b| a.scope.cmp(&b.scope).then(a.name.cmp(&b.name)));
  1039	            Some(NamesSection { entries })
  1040	        }
  1041	    };
  1042	
  1043	    // **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** serialize custom FormatTable entries
  1044	    // (id ≥ FIRST_CUSTOM_FORMAT_ID). Built-ins are re-seeded from
  1045	    // `FormatTable::default()` on load and aren't persisted. None if the
  1046	    // table has no custom entries (typical for built-in-only workbooks).
  1047	    let formats_section = {
  1048	        let mut entries: Vec<FormatEntry> = wb
  1049	            .formats()
  1050	            .iter()
  1051	            .filter(|(id, _)| id.0 >= ql_storage::FIRST_CUSTOM_FORMAT_ID)
  1052	            .map(|(id, s)| FormatEntry {
  1053	                id: id.0,
  1054	                string: s.to_owned(),
  1055	            })
  1056	            .collect();
  1057	        if entries.is_empty() {
  1058	            None
  1059	        } else {
  1060	            entries.sort_by_key(|e| e.id);
  1061	            Some(FormatsSection { entries })
  1062	        }
  1063	    };
  1064	
  1065	    let envelope = WorkbookEnvelope {
  1066	        schema_version: WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  1067	        name: name.to_owned(),
  1068	        sheets: sheet_envelopes,
  1069	        names: names_section,
  1070	        // **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** persist the workbook's date system.
  1071	        date_system: Some(DateSystemWire::from_runtime(wb.date_system())),
  1072	        // **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** persist custom format entries.
  1073	        formats: formats_section,
  1074	    };
  1075	    let toml_str = toml::to_string_pretty(&envelope)?;
  1076	    fs::write(dir.join("workbook.toml"), toml_str)?;
  1077	
  1078	    // Per-sheet JSONL.
  1079	    for sheet_id in 0..(wb.sheet_count() as SheetId) {
  1080	        let sheet = wb.sheet(sheet_id).expect("sheet_count was wrong");
  1081	        let sheet_path = sheets_dir.join(format!("{sheet_id}.jsonl"));
  1082	        let f = fs::File::create(&sheet_path)?;
  1083	        let mut writer = BufWriter::new(f);
  1084	        let bounds = sheet.bounds();
  1085	
  1086	        // Collect formula cells for this sheet so we can emit them even when their
  1087	        // row/col falls outside the value-bounds rectangle (e.g. a formula cell
  1088	        // with no evaluated value yet, or a formula at an extreme position).
  1089	        // Sort by (row, col) for deterministic output.
  1090	        let mut formula_positions: Vec<(RowId, ColId)> = wb
  1091	            .iter_formulas()
  1092	            .filter(|(s, _, _, _)| *s == sheet_id)
  1093	            .map(|(_, r, c, _)| (r, c))
  1094	            .collect();
  1095	        formula_positions.sort();
  1096	
  1097	        // Track which (row, col) pairs we've already written from the value-bounds
  1098	        // sweep, so the formula-only pass doesn't double-emit.
  1099	        let mut written: std::collections::HashSet<(RowId, ColId)> =
  1100	            std::collections::HashSet::new();
  1101	
  1102	        // Naive iteration over value bounds. Sparse cells (Blank without a formula)
  1103	        // are skipped. Phase 2 may optimize via direct chunk-walking; not on hot path.
  1104	        for row in 0..bounds.row_extent {
  1105	            for col in 0..bounds.col_extent {
  1106	                let v = sheet.read(row, col);
  1107	                // Audit M6 fix (2026-05-12): reject NaN/Inf at the save boundary.
  1108	                if let Value::Number(n) = v {
  1109	                    if !n.is_finite() {
  1110	                        return Err(QbookError::NonFiniteNumber {
  1111	                            sheet: sheet_id,
  1112	                            row,
  1113	                            col,
  1114	                            value: n,
  1115	                        });
  1116	                    }
  1117	                }
  1118	                let wire = CellWireValue::from_value(&v);
  1119	                let formula = wb
  1120	                    .formula_at(sheet_id, row, col)
  1121	                    .map(|s| s.as_ref().to_owned());
  1122	                // Emit if there's a non-blank value OR a formula.
  1123	                if wire.is_some() || formula.is_some() {
  1124	                    // Phase 2A.8 audit M11: formula-bearing cells with Blank
  1125	                    // saved value encode as `Pending` (was: `Error(#NULL!)`,
  1126	                    // which conflated "not yet evaluated" with a real Excel
  1127	                    // #NULL! error). `Pending` is a distinct wire variant the
  1128	                    // loader recognizes as "recompute required."
  1129	                    let rec = CellRecord {
  1130	                        row,
  1131	                        col,
  1132	                        value: wire.unwrap_or(CellWireValue::Pending),
  1133	                        formula,
  1134	                    };
  1135	                    let line = serde_json::to_string(&rec)?;
  1136	                    writeln!(writer, "{line}")?;
  1137	                    written.insert((row, col));
  1138	                }
  1139	            }
  1140	        }
  1141	
  1142	        // Emit formula cells whose (row, col) fell outside the value-bounds
  1143	        // rectangle. These are formula-only cells with Blank values: encoded
  1144	        // as `Pending` per Phase 2A.8 audit M11 so the formula text survives
  1145	        // round-trip without conflating with a real #NULL! error.
  1146	        for (row, col) in formula_positions {
  1147	            if written.contains(&(row, col)) {
  1148	                continue;
  1149	            }
  1150	            let formula = wb
  1151	                .formula_at(sheet_id, row, col)
  1152	                .map(|s| s.as_ref().to_owned());
  1153	            let rec = CellRecord {
  1154	                row,
  1155	                col,
  1156	                value: CellWireValue::Pending,
  1157	                formula,
  1158	            };
  1159	            let line = serde_json::to_string(&rec)?;
  1160	            writeln!(writer, "{line}")?;
  1161	        }
  1162	        writer.flush()?;
  1163	    }
  1164	
  1165	    // Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 H1: write the atomic-save marker LAST, after
  1166	    // envelope + all sheet JSONLs. A partial write that fails before this
  1167	    // point leaves a directory WITHOUT the marker, so recovery won't
  1168	    // mistake the partial result for an engine-owned backup. Marker
  1169	    // content is the schema version + a human-readable note (no parsing —
  1170	    // the filename's presence is the only signal recovery consults).
  1171	    fs::write(
  1172	        dir.join(ATOMIC_SAVE_MARKER_FILENAME),
  1173	        format!(
  1174	            "Quantbook atomic-save marker v1 (schema_version={WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION}).\n\
  1175	             Presence of this file proves the directory was written by the\n\
  1176	             engine's atomic-save protocol. Used by recover_from_crashed_save\n\
  1177	             to distinguish engine-owned `.bak-<random>` siblings from\n\
  1178	             user-created sibling directories. Safe to ignore.\n"
  1179	        ),
  1180	    )?;

   190	    /// Note on bound-plan staleness: bound plans capture the workbook's
   191	    /// NameTable state at buffer time. Names registered between two
   192	    /// transactions take effect for the next transaction's `put_formula` calls,
   193	    /// but cannot be retroactively rebound (the transaction holds `&mut
   194	    /// Workbook` for its lifetime, so the user can't mutate names mid-tx
   195	    /// anyway).
   196	    pub fn put_formula(
   197	        &mut self,
   198	        sheet: SheetId,
   199	        row: RowId,
   200	        col: ColId,

   203	        validate_cell(self.workbook, sheet, row, col)?;
   204	        self.check_op_kind(sheet, row, col, OpKind::Formula)?;
   205

   207	        let tokens = lex(text.as_ref())?;
   208	        let expr = parse(tokens)?;
   209	        // Bind eagerly against the current workbook NameTable. The transaction
   210	        // doesn't allow registering names mid-batch (the &mut Workbook borrow
   211	        // prevents the user from mutating names while the tx is alive), so the
   212	        // table is stable for the transaction's lifetime — eager binding is
   213	        // safe and surfaces UnresolvedName / UnsupportedVariant before any
   214	        // writes land.
   215	        // W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D): pass `&Workbook` for names so the two-tier

   220	            sheet,

   222	            col,
   223	            text,
   224	            plan,
   225	        });
   226	        Ok(())
   227	    }

docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:35:IronCalc's test corpus includes `SUM(Sheet1!3:$3)`, `SUM('Second Sheet'!C:D)`, and `SUM(Sheet2!D4:C20)`. Those are the common forms the doc references, but Decision B only defines `sheet_qualifier? (cell_ref | range_ref)`. The current AST has one `sheet: Option<SheetId>` per `RangeRef`, so the parser must define how `Sheet2!D4:C20` is represented: the qualifier applies to the entire range, including the unqualified right endpoint. Current `build_range` style would otherwise combine `CellRef(Some(sheet))` with `CellRef(None)` and either drop the sheet or reject the range unless changed deliberately.

  1193	/// Load a `Workbook` from a `.qbook/` directory.
  1194	///
  1195	/// Phase 2A.8 audit H2: before reading, calls `recover_from_crashed_save` to

  1197	/// save. Recovery either rolls forward (target valid, cleanup orphan) or rolls
  1198	/// back (target missing/invalid, restore orphan).
  1199	pub fn load_workbook(path: &Path) -> Result<Workbook, QbookError> {
  1200	    // Recovery happens BEFORE the directory check — if the prior save crashed
  1201	    // between step 2 (rename → bak) and step 3 (rename temp → target), the
  1202	    // target may not yet exist. Recovery restores it from .bak.
  1203	    recover_from_crashed_save(path)?;
  1204	
  1205	    if !path.is_dir() {
  1206	        return Err(QbookError::NotADirectory {

  1279	        // **W5-84 closure (Codex MEDIUM-3):** the loader now VALIDATES
  1280	        // that each overlay id resolves in the workbook's `FormatTable`,
  1281	        // matching the runtime + replay contract (which both surface
  1282	        // `UnknownFormatId` / `FormatNotRegistered` on bad ids). A
  1283	        // corrupted `.qbook` binding a cell to an unregistered id no

  1286	        if let Some(ref overlay_entries) = sheet_env.format_overlay {

  1288	            // mutation so we don't borrow `wb` twice (mutable on the

  1290	            let known_ids: std::collections::HashSet<u32> =

  1296	                // `MalformedFormatOverlay` rather than silently binding

  1302	                        col: entry.col,
  1303	                        why: "out-of-range row/col",
  1304	                    });
  1305	                }
  1306	                if !known_ids.contains(&entry.id) {
  1307	                    return Err(QbookError::MalformedFormatOverlay {
  1308	                        sheet: sheet_id,
  1309	                        row: entry.row,
  1310	                        col: entry.col,
  1311	                        why: "format id not registered in FormatTable",
  1312	                    });
  1313	                }
  1314	                sheet_mut.format_overlay_mut().set(
  1315	                    entry.row,
  1316	                    entry.col,
  1317	                    ql_storage::FormatId(entry.id),
  1318	                );
  1319	            }
  1320	        }
  1321	
  1322	        // Audit M2 fix (2026-05-12): a missing sheet JSONL is now an explicit
  1323	        // MissingFile error rather than silently treating the sheet as empty
  1324	        // (which would mask corruption / partial saves). The on-disk format
  1325	        // emits an empty file for an empty sheet, so file-presence is the
  1326	        // correctness oracle.
  1327	        let sheet_path = sheets_dir.join(format!("{sheet_id}.jsonl"));
  1328	        if !sheet_path.is_file() {
  1329	            return Err(QbookError::MissingFile { file: sheet_path });

  1331	        let f = fs::File::open(&sheet_path)?;
  1332	        let reader = BufReader::new(f);
  1333	        for (line_no, line) in reader.lines().enumerate() {

  1336	                continue;

  1343	                })?;
